### 2026-09-07 08:44:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Stock Market #Importance 2

Japanese Shares Jump on Tech Boost — The Nikkei 225 Index jumped 2% to above 66,300 while the broader Topix Index gained 0.8% to 4,136 on Monday, with Japanese shares advancing for a second straight session as technology and artificial intelligence-linked stocks outperformed. The gains came as US chipmakers and memory stocks rallied on Friday amid improving sentiment toward the sector, although the broader market remained pressured by stronger-than-expected US jobs data that boosted expectations for a Federal Reserve rate hike this month. Markets also priced in a rate increase from the Bank of Japan at its September meeting as the country continues to grapple with persistent inflation and a weak yen. Top tech gainers included Kioxia Holdings (7.2%), SoftBank Group (6.1%), Advantest (2.6%), Fujikura (5.9%) and Tokyo Electron (5.3%). Meanwhile, financial and consumer stocks underperformed, with losses from Mitsubishi UFJ (-0.9%) and Nintendo (-2.2%).

【Six ministries issue Opinions on Promoting High-Quality Development of the Historical Classics Industry】China's Ministry of Industry and Information Technology (MIIT) and five other ministries issued Opinions on Promoting High-Quality Development of the Historical Classics Industry, setting explicit targets to scale the sector. ### 2026-09-07 08:33:50  #國際

【反親日熱潮在韓國集中爆發】曾祖父有親日行徑的韓國女演員安賀營（音）將不會出演奈飛原創韓國醫療劇《外傷重症中心》的下一季。此次事件讓韓國國內清算親日派的行動再次受到關注。近期，韓國從民間到政府都出現了加大清算親日派的勢頭。在民間，韓國民眾特別是年輕人透過各類活動來表達對親日勢力的抵制。在政府層面，韓國宣佈將調查、追繳親日反民族行為者不當聚斂的財產，並追究其歷史責任。有分析認為，韓國年輕人現在將親日相關問題放在社會公平框架下重新解讀，而政府行動已經超出追繳親日財產本身，進一步觸及韓國社會如何認識殖民歷史、如何補償獨立運動有功者後代以及由誰掌握歷史解釋權的問題。（環球時報）

【高盛建議做空歐元兌澳元 看好澳元跑贏其他貨幣】高盛預計，歐元兌澳元將跌至 2023 年初以來最低水平。歐洲正應對能源價格衝擊，而澳元受益於有利的全球宏觀環境。由卡馬克什亞・特里韋迪（Kamakshya Trivedi）牽頭的策略師團隊在研報中寫道：“全球經濟穩健增長，能源與金屬領域貿易條件再度迎來利好，財政韌性突出，同時 G10 國家中最高政策利率帶來可觀的息差收益，以上因素均將支撐澳元。”與此同時，歐洲依舊處於 “全球能源衝擊遺留風險的中心”，貿易條件承壓，歐元面臨挑戰。“我們認為後續能源因素會帶來歐元適度走弱的風險，疊加歐元本土層面擾動有限，使其成為包括澳元在內貨幣極具吸引力的融資貨幣。”建議做空歐元兌澳元，目標位 1.5750，止損位 1.6520。

### 2026-09-07 08:29:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Israel #Tourist Arrivals #Importance 0

Israel Tourist Arrivals Fall in August — The number of tourist arrivals in Israel fell 1.9 percent year-on-year to 115.9 thousand in August 2026, reversing a 28.6% surge in the previous month. Total visitor arrivals declined to 117.6 thousand from 119.6 thousand, while day visitors increased to 1.7 thousand from 1.3 thousand. In January-August, tourist arrivals totaled 668.2 thousand, down from 827.5 thousand in the same period of 2025. The US accounted for the largest share of arrivals at 34.9 percent, followed by France at 13.1 percent, the UK at 7.9 percent and Russia at 4.8 percent.

【經濟日報：17億塊晶片的喜與憂】7月份，我國規模以上工業企業積體電路產量達530億塊，相當於日均生產約17億塊晶片；今年前7個月，我國積體電路出口總額達2160億美元，同比增長99.5%，已超2025年全年2019億美元的出口總額。這組資料表明，中國積體電路產業正迎來新一輪高速增長。欣喜之餘，我們仍需清醒看到行業隱憂。憂在何處？產業深層矛盾尚待破解。技術壁壘與“卡脖子”風險猶存。儘管晶片產量與出口飆升，但高階晶片仍受制於國外技術封鎖，關鍵材料、裝置國產化率不足，產業鏈安全存在隱患。產業結構隱憂顯現。當前產能增長主要依賴儲存晶片與成熟製程，中低端晶片產能集中、存在重複建設問題，高階晶片產能不足、存在結構性緊缺。國際競爭壓力持續加劇。美國對華技術圍堵不斷升級，全球半導體格局重塑充滿不確定性，出口高增長能否持續面臨考驗。面對機遇與挑戰並存的格局，中國積體電路產業需從“量的積累”轉向“質的突破”。錨定高階，突破“卡脖子”瓶頸。要持續加大研發投入，把資源向關鍵核心技術傾斜，聚焦裝置、材料等薄弱環節集中攻關，用好新型舉國體制，推動單項技術突破向全鏈條能力躍升。

【Japan's FX reserves record largest monthly drop in August】Japan's FX reserves posted a record monthly decline in August after Tokyo launched a fresh, large-scale dollar-selling/yen-buying intervention to counter yen weakness, the Finance Ministry said. Reserves fell to $1.208 tln at end-August from $1.287 tln a month earlier, a record drop of $79.6 bln, or 6.18%. The fall was driven largely by a sharp reduction in foreign-currency securities holdings, much of which are US Treasuries bought during dollar-buying interventions about 20 years ago and which account for roughly 70% of Japan's FX reserves.

### 2026-09-07 08:20:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Tunisia #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Tunisia Inflation Rate Hits 3-Month High of 5.4% — Tunisia's annual inflation rate accelerated for the first time in three months to 5.4% in August 2026, from July’s four-month low of 5.1%. It was the highest inflation rate since May, mainly driven by faster rises in food & non-alcoholic beverages (7.5% vs 6.6% in July) and transportation prices (2.1% vs 2%). Prices also rose at a faster pace for health (3.4% vs 2.7%) and restaurants & hotels (6.6% vs 6.5%), while clothing & footwear prices eased slightly (9.0% vs 9.1%). Meanwhile, core inflation, which excludes food and energy, inched up to 4.9% from 4.8% in July. On a monthly basis, the CPI climbed by 0.5%, accelerating from a 0.2% rise in the previous month and marking the fastest increase in four months.

【中信證券：當前美債期限利差結構的變化總體仍屬“正常化”而非失控】中信證券研報稱，本輪發達市場長債上行並非單一通脹衝擊，而是實際利率、期限溢價、財政擴與央行政策同步重估的結果。當前美債期限利差結構的變化總體仍屬“正常化”而非失控，若美聯儲9月如期加息，且美國通脹與長債供給壓力延續，10年期、30年期美債收益率或分別向5.0%和5.5%靠攏，短期將繼續壓制高估值資產，中長期拐點仍取決於美國通脹回落速度以及財政鞏固政策。2015年以來的兩輪美聯儲加息週期，香港市場資金流入通常自加息前四周起逐步放緩，在加息當週降至低點，隨後逐步修復。但若全球長端利率繼續上行，港股高股息相對海外無風險資產的收益率優勢有所收窄，配置上應進一步考察現金流穩定性、盈利確定性和分紅可持續性，並關注南向持倉佔比較高、外資邊際定價權相對較低的細分方向，建議優先關注銀行、煤炭、物業管理和燃氣。

Silver Holds Decline on Fed Rate Hike Bets — Silver traded near $66 an ounce on Monday after losing more than 1% in the previous session, pressured by growing expectations of an imminent Federal Reserve interest rate hike following stronger-than-expected US jobs data last week. Data released Friday showed US nonfarm payrolls rose by 162,000 in August, following an upwardly revised increase of 23,000 in July and well above market expectations for a gain of 56,000. Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remained unchanged at 4.1%, while annual wage growth slowed to 3.1%, although the decline was smaller than economists had expected. Markets increased bets on a September Fed rate hike to roughly 60%, up from around 50% before the data release. Silver also remained under pressure from higher oil prices as the US and Iran exchanged strikes on ships over the weekend, stoking concerns about renewed inflationary pressures.

【俄稱日本有病別傳染給他國】俄羅斯外交部發言人扎哈羅娃9月6日直斥日本首相高市早苗關於“拆除俄羅斯一座紀念碑”的要求太荒謬。 俄羅斯遠東哈巴羅夫斯克市9月4日為一座紀念戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑舉行揭幕儀式。日本首相高市早苗隨後在社交媒體平臺用日語和俄語發帖，要求俄方將紀念碑拆除。高市早苗在帖文中稱，“菊花紋章作為象徵日本的紋章被廣泛認知，設立表現其被摧毀場景的紀念碑完全無法接受。這是關乎日本國民感情的問題，日本政府此前已就此向俄方強烈要求停止設定並拆除該紀念碑。今後將繼續強烈敦促其拆除。” 據俄新社9月6日報道，扎哈羅娃就此發問，日本政府“接下來是不是該要求風不要吹、太陽不要發光了？” 扎哈羅娃還強調， “我們的歷史基於事實，我們的記憶鐫刻於紀念碑上。日方無視本國曆史，但別把這種‘病’傳染給其他國家。” 這是俄羅斯目前最大的、紀念戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑，由俄羅斯軍事歷史學會與哈巴羅夫斯克邊疆區政府合作建成。紀念碑中央是一尊15米高的、手持長槍的蘇聯士兵雕像，旁邊一座繪有菊花圖案的界碑被砸成兩半。俄羅斯軍事歷史學會表示，被搗毀的菊花圖案正是軍國主義日本的象徵，如此設計紀念碑是正確和符合史實的。（CCTV國際時訊）

Earnings: aggregate Hong Kong-listed profit growth in 1H2026 rebounded sharply YoY but is highly divergent — high-dividend earnings turned positive mainly on resource-sector strength (sustainability uncertain), innovative drugs sustained strong growth, while internet profit declines widened. 【經濟日報金觀平：用好涉稅資訊規範平臺經濟】稅務部門最新資料顯示，《網際網路平臺企業涉稅資訊報送規定》實施一年多來，近9700家境內外平臺企業積極履行涉稅資訊報送義務，平臺經濟規範健康發展邁出堅實一步。長期以來，由於交易虛擬性、資料不透明、資金流轉複雜等特點，網際網路平臺滋生出刷單炒信、不實申報等違規行為。一些地方還利用平臺企業“註冊地與經營地分離”的資訊壁壘，以“開票經濟”虛增收入，妨礙全國統一大市場建設。新規實施一年多來，制度監管下的合規行為正逐步轉化為網際網路平臺及平臺內經營者的內在自覺。與去年10月首次報送時相比，今年一季度報送涉稅資訊的平臺數量增長39%，報送的平臺內經營者和從業人員資訊分別增長21%、19%。用好資料，重點在“精準”。要透過多部門涉稅資訊共享和協同共治，進一步實現對涉稅違法行為從事後打擊向事前事中精準防範轉變，合力提升稅收服務與管理效能，有力有效保護納稅人合法權益。利用海量資料完善風險模型，實時掃描異常並主動提醒，加強對苗頭性、傾向性問題的研判和防範。針對“開票經濟”亂象，強化部門聯動，打破資訊壁壘，透過交叉比對多維資料、建立動態風險指標模型、強化聯合懲戒等措施，精準查處“空轉走單”“迴圈開票”等違規行為，擠出數字水分，為高質量發展營造公平健康環境。

### 2026-09-07 07:56:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Foreign Exchange Reserves #Importance 2

Japan Forex Reserves Hit Nearly 4-Year Low — Japan’s foreign reserves sank by USD 79.58 billion to USD 1.208 trillion at the end of August 2026 from USD 1.287 trillion in July, the lowest level since October 2022, as large-scale yen-buying intervention reduced foreign-currency assets. The Ministry of Finance reported ¥15.4 trillion of foreign-exchange intervention between July 30 and August 26 as authorities moved to support the yen after it weakened sharply. Foreign currency reserves stood at USD 994.98 billion, consisting of USD 839.56 billion in securities and USD 155.42 billion in deposits. Of the deposits, USD 154.98 billion were held with foreign central banks and the Bank for International Settlements (BIS), while deposits with banks headquartered in Japan totaled USD 409 million and those with banks headquartered outside Japan amounted to USD 25 million. Japan's IMF reserve position stood at USD 11.42 billion, SDR holdings were valued at USD 61.17 billion, and gold reserves totaled USD 124.10 billion.

【“六張網”建設密集推進 下半年實物工作量集中釋放】近期，“六張網”建設迎來政策密集落地訊號。8月27日，《物流網建設實施方案》正式印發，成為六網體系中首個對外發布的國家級專項實施方案，標誌著“六張網”從頂層設計全面轉入分網施工、分批落地的攻堅階段。8月28日召開的全國投資工作推進會議和9月2日召開的“六張網”重大專案協調推進機制會，接連釋放加快新型政策性金融工具投放、抓緊形成實物工作量的政策訊號。國家發展改革委國家資訊中心經濟預測部政策模擬實驗室主任、研究員肖宏偉向證券時報記者表示，從落地節奏看，今年三季度與四季度將呈現差異化安排。三季度是施工黃金期，地方層面將加快在建專案進度、推動擬建專案儘早落地；四季度則將迎來重大專案集中開工的高峰期，對總需求的帶動作用更加明顯。隨著資金撥付逐步到位、前期儲備專案成熟度提升，更多實物工作量將在下半年集中釋放。全年超7萬億元級的投資盤子裡，超六成工作量有望集中在下半年兌現。

Brent Rises as US and Iran Exchange Fire — Brent crude rose toward $97 per barrel on Monday, extending last week’s gains as the US and Iran exchanged strikes in the Middle East, fueling concerns over prolonged disruptions to energy flows from the region. The US targeted three Iranian oil tankers over the weekend in retaliation for ballistic missile attacks on US Navy warships. In response, Tehran said a new restricted zone would be established outside the Strait of Hormuz, including a US Navy blockade line and extending into parts of the Persian Gulf. Meanwhile, US Energy Secretary Chris Wright said the US would maintain its naval presence in the Middle East, including the blockade designed to curb Iranian oil exports and help ensure the safe passage of commercial vessels through Hormuz. Fighting between the two sides resumed last week after roughly a month of relative calm, pushing oil prices about 10% higher as markets reassessed geopolitical and supply risks.

【2026年基金半年報首次披露新指標，42只“長跑基”讓九成基民賺到錢】2026年基金半年報首次披露了“近一年盈利投資者數量佔比”這一衡量基民持有體驗的新指標。資料顯示，全市場主動權益基金中，該佔比超90%的基金僅佔47.73%。 證券時報基金研究院將範圍縮小至公募行業金字塔尖的“雙十”基金經理，即擔任基金經理年限超10年（截至9月4日，下同）、管理基金綜合年化收益率超10%（共管產品以排在第一位的基金經理為準）的群體。在這些基金經理管理的52只近10年年化收益率超10%的“長跑基”中，有42只基金近一年盈利投資者數量佔比超過90%，佔比高達80.8%，遠超全市場整體水平。 這些持有體驗較佳的基金涉及30多位基金經理，各有各的長跑之道：有人靠“長情”，以低換手和穩定持倉讓基民看得懂、拿得住；有人靠剋制，以極致分散和嚴控回撤讓基民拿得穩、不心慌；有人靠敏銳，以產業週期的精準把握捕捉景氣向上的機會。（人民財訊）

### 2026-09-07 07:23:57  #公司

【近期南非連續發生涉南僑胞被綁架案件，中使館提醒提高安全防護意識】據“中國駐南非大使館”公眾號訊息，近期，南非約翰內斯堡的塞爾比、傑米斯頓地區連續發生涉我僑胞被綁架案件。綁匪以經營店鋪、酒鋪的僑胞為主要目標，以索要贖金為作案目的，選擇受害人送貨途中或門店附近作案。我僑胞人身財產安全受到嚴重威脅。駐南非使館提醒廣大僑胞，務必密切關注當地安全和治安形勢，進一步提高安全防護意識，主動防範被綁架風險：一、加強安防措施。儘量選擇安保設施齊全、周邊環境安全的社群經營居住。已在較高風險地區從業僑胞儘快搬離，加大店鋪周圍和出行途中安防措施，運用遠端監控等手段看店，配置“一鍵報警”等行動式防範裝置手段，加強與親友和鄰居日常聯絡。提前規劃安全避險和脫離路線，降低被綁架風險。二、確保出行安全。合理調整店鋪營業時間，避免清晨夜間單獨出行。時常變換通勤及進出貨路線和時間，進出經營居住場所和行車出行時多留意周邊環境和陌生人，防止遭人尾隨。如發現尾隨情形，迅速駕車前往附近警局或加油站等相對安全場所，並視情向華人社團求助。避免前往綁架案件安全風險高、社會治安不靖地段。三、依法合規經營。遵守當地法律，儘量僱傭當地人，確保合法用工，加強對員工及臨時僱員的情況瞭解和日常觀察，妥善處理勞資關係。不隨意透露自己的住址、行程、車輛、作息和財務資訊，避免因非法用工經營引發案件。不隨身攜帶大量現金，不在公共場合談論公司經營、貨款往來和家庭財產情況。四、沉著冷靜應對。如遇到危險，儘量保持冷靜，以人身安全為重，找準時機報警或逃脫。如無法逃脫，不與綁匪衝突、不透露家底、不允諾贖金，相機靈活應對，以便警方營救。受害人家屬應及時報警，並聽從警方談判專家意見，不輕易許諾高額贖金，主動向警方提供相關細節，積極配合警方開展偵查，爭取儘快解救被綁架人員。駐南非使館將繼續推動南非警方儘快偵辦涉中國公民案件，切實維護中國公民安全和合法權益。

李書福還表示：汽車產業是一個非常嚴肅的產業，營造公平透明的競爭氛圍對產業的健康發展至關重要。做得好不如說得好，這確實傷害了幹實事人的感情。吉利堅持一切經營活動都不得觸碰法律的紅線和道德的底線，嚴格規定不能有水軍，不能表面一套背後一套，這是企業的底線。（人民日報）

【特朗普社媒驚現“4小時27帖”大刷屏：魔改伊朗地圖、宣稱擁有月球】美東時間週日中午至下午4時許，現任美國總統唐納德·特朗普在其創辦的社交媒體平臺 Truth Social 上再次開啟了罕見的“高頻刷屏”模式，在短短約4個小時內高調發布了多達27條內容，其中包含大量由人工智慧（AI）生成的諷刺與政治暗示圖片。根據海外媒體披露的動態詳情，特朗普此輪發布的27條動態主題五花八門，涵蓋了國際政治、改名領土、自我神化以及對國內政敵的猛烈炮轟。

國際摩擦與地緣宣示： 特朗普在針對中東局勢的動態中，釋出了一張將伊朗國家地圖魔改成自己側臉的AI圖片，並一度將該圖設為其個人頭像。此外，他還發布了多張轟炸伊朗原油樞紐哈爾克島、美軍繳獲油輪的AI虛擬畫面。更令人瞠目結舌的是，他還上傳了一張月球照片，並簡單粗暴地配文宣稱“月球是我們的”。

改名熱潮與政策宣導： 在涉及本土及周邊地理內容中，特朗普放出圖片建議將新墨西哥州更名為“新美國”，並利用AI影象表達了對重新命名安大略湖的意圖。在展示國家武裝力量方面，他高頻釋出多張由AI渲染的“美國太空軍制服樣式圖”及自己身穿黑色極簡科幻制服指揮外太空海戰的圖集。

自吹自擂與政敵嘲諷： 特朗普在此輪動態中堅稱自己“將被作為歷史上最偉大的總統而銘記”。而在抨擊民主黨方面，他則直接使用了一張AI生成的、標有“民主黨”字樣的泰坦尼克號沉沒影象來表達對政敵的看衰。

值得一提的是，就在此番瘋狂刷屏發生前，特朗普的社媒賬號剛剛上線了“獨家內容優先訪問權（Premium Access）”功能，訂購定價高達每月10萬美元。外界普遍猜測，此輪極其荒誕卻極具流量效應的“AI圖片大爆發”，或許與其進一步將其社媒影響力“變現”的商業目的密切相關。目前，白宮和相關發言人尚未對總統這一極具個人風格、虛實結合的“社媒風暴”發表正式評論。

### 2026-09-07 07:06:47  #國際

【中美戰區司令會晤釋放的訊號，臺灣要聽懂】根據2026年5月美國總統特朗普訪華時的雙方共識，習近平主席將於今年秋季對美國進行國事訪問。進入9月，中美互動備受關注。對臺灣更是如此。 據新加坡《聯合早報》報道，8月31日至9 月2日，第28屆印太國防軍司令會議在加拿大維多利亞舉行。會議期間，中國人民解放軍東部戰區司令員楊志斌與美軍太平洋司令部司令帕帕羅舉行首次雙邊會談。雙方討論保持兩軍高層溝通暢通的重要性，以降低誤判風險，並推動雙方部隊以安全、專業方式行動。 這場會面之所以格外引人注目，關鍵在於雙方的身份。東部戰區直接擔負臺海方向的戰略任務；太平洋司令部則是美國在西太平洋進行軍事部署、推進盟友協作和介入地區事務的重要中樞。 兩位司令的會面，代表中美兩軍在最敏感方向恢復戰區層級面對面溝通。這意味著雙方正在最容易發生摩擦的領域，建立必要的聯絡與管控，為防止局勢失控設定底線。臺灣真正該聽懂什麼 中美戰區司令會晤，對臺灣釋放了三層清晰訊號。 第一，臺海不能成為中美戰略誤判的引爆點。中美都不願因為軍事摩擦而直接衝突，這為地區穩定增加了一層緩衝。 第二，臺海更不能成為 “臺獨” 挑釁的試驗場。中美兩軍溝通是為了管控風險，絕不是為了容忍風險製造者不斷突破紅線。 第三，臺海和平的根基不在外部承諾，而在兩岸關係本身。外部溝通可以減少危險，卻不能解決臺灣問題；真正能為臺灣同胞帶來長久安寧的，是堅持一箇中國原則、反對“臺獨”分裂、推動兩岸恢復交流與對話，祖國統一才是臺海和平之根。 （環球網）

【U.S. attack on Iranian vessels raises escalation risk; oil opens higher】Brent briefly rose about 0.8% at the open to above $95/bbl and WTI topped $90/bbl after a U.S. strike on Iranian tankers and Tehran’s announcement of a new exclusion zone outside the Strait of Hormuz heightened fears of prolonged energy transit disruption. The U.S. said it struck three Iranian tankers over the weekend, destroying one, in retaliation for Iran’s ballistic missile attack on a U.S. naval vessel. Iran’s top security official said a new exclusion zone will be set in coming days, starting from the U.S. naval blockade line and extending into parts of the Persian Gulf. U.S. Energy Secretary Wright said the U.S. Navy will not ease deployments in the Strait, including blocking Iranian oil exports and ensuring safe passage for other merchant vessels. Brent is up nearly 60% year-to-date; refined fuels such as diesel have risen more amid the Middle East conflict and the Russia‑Iran war.

### 2026-09-07 06:51:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Stock Market #Importance 1

NZX 50 Trades Slightly Lower to Start the Week — New Zealand stocks dropped 21 points, or 0.2%, to 13,954 in Monday morning trade, after gaining in the previous session, tracking a decline on Wall Street on Friday amid expectations that the Fed would raise interest rates at its upcoming meeting following stronger-than-expected labor data. Traders were cautious ahead of China’s CPI and PPI data, as well as trade figures for August, due later this week. Investors also monitored developments in the Middle East conflict after Tehran said it had targeted oil tankers in response to US strikes. Traders also continued to assess Friday's statement from RBNZ Assistant Governor Karen Silk, which signaled that New Zealand’s central bank is more likely to wait until December before raising interest rates again. The tech, consumer discretionary, energy, and financial sectors mainly weighed on the index, with early losers including Ryman Healthcare (-1.0%), ANZ Group (-0.8%), Infratil (-0.8%), and Channel Infrastructure (-0.6%).

【Iran to raise gasoline price for heavy users from Tuesday】Iran will raise the gasoline price for consumers using more than 110 liters per month, effective Tuesday, government spokesman Mohajerani said. First- and second-tier quotas, including the 110‑liter band, will remain unchanged; the third-tier price will increase to 100,000 Iranian rials per liter (about $0.04 at the free-market exchange rate). Mohajerani said the adjustment, driven by the “current situation” including the war and US sanctions, will only affect monthly users above 110 liters. Other motorists can still buy up to 60 liters at 15,000 rials/liter and an additional 50 liters at 30,000 rials/liter. Iran’s gasoline remains among the cheapest globally; authorities previously delayed hikes over fears of sparking protests after 2019 unrest.

【AfD posts large lead in Saxony‑Anhalt state election】Sept 6 — Exit poll in Saxony‑Anhalt state election shows Alternative for Germany (AfD) 44.5%, versus 20.8% in 2021; the CDU led by Merz falls to 18.5% from 37.1% in 2021. Initial seat projection for the 83‑seat state parliament gives AfD 39 seats, three short of an absolute majority. Other mainstream parties have ruled out joining an AfD‑led coalition, leaving AfD’s entry into the state government uncertain even if it becomes the largest party. 【德國薩安州選舉極右翼政黨大幅領先】9月6日，德國東部薩克森-安哈爾特州（薩安州）舉行州議會選舉。最新出口民調顯示，極右翼的德國選擇黨得票率44.5%，較2021年的20.8%大幅躍升。德國總理默茨所在的基民盟得票率從2021年的37.1%驟降至18.5%。這對自2002年以來一直執掌該州的基民盟而言是一次重挫。 首輪議席預測顯示，薩安州議會共83席，德國選擇黨預計將獲得39席，距離單獨執政所需的絕對多數席位還差3席。由於德國其他傳統主流政黨此前均已排除加入由德國選擇黨領導的執政聯盟的可能性，如果目前的議席格局最終得到確認，該黨即使成為州議會第一大黨，能否進入州政府仍存在較大不確定性。 德國選擇黨方面表示，將向“所有希望推行更好政策的有生力量”伸出合作之手，同時強調移民和國內安全等議題是不可談判的底線。該黨領導人魏德爾稱，該黨擁有“明確的執政授權”，將與任何能在政策立場上找到共識的理性政黨展開對話。 與基民盟聯合執政的社民黨聯合主席、德國副總理兼財長克林拜爾表示，薩安州選舉結果是對聯邦政府的一個警示，值得聯邦政府反思其政策。 本月，德國東北部的梅克倫堡-前波莫瑞州也將舉行地方選舉，德國選擇黨的民調支援率也位居第一。（CCTV國際時訊）

【俄外交部 ：高市要求俄拆除紀念碑的言論“荒謬且無視歷史”】俄羅斯外交部發言人扎哈羅娃6日回應日本首相高市早苗要求俄拆除戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑言論時說，這如同要求“不要颳風和太陽停止發光”一樣荒謬，不要把無視本國曆史的“病”傳染給其他國家。俄遠東哈巴羅夫斯克市4日為一座紀念戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑舉行揭幕儀式。該紀念碑是俄目前最大的紀念戰勝軍國主義日本的紀念碑，由俄羅斯軍事歷史學會與哈巴羅夫斯克邊疆區政府合作建成。紀念碑中央是一尊15米高的蘇聯士兵雕像，代表擊敗日本關東軍主力的蘇聯紅軍。士兵手持長槍，旁邊一座繪有菊花圖案的界碑被砸成兩半。扎哈羅娃6日表示：“我們的領土由先輩守護下來，我們的歷史基於事實，我們的記憶鐫刻在紀念碑上。”扎哈羅娃特別指出，日本方面無視本國曆史，別把這種“病”傳染給其他國家。俄國家杜馬（議會下院）議員奧列格·馬特維切夫稱，二戰是以日本無條件投降結束的，哈巴羅夫斯克的紀念碑正是為此設立，在這一大背景下，日方要求拆除這座紀念碑“很可笑”。俄羅斯軍事歷史學會副主席奧夫西延科表示，該紀念碑上被搗毀的菊花圖案，正是軍國主義日本的象徵，如此設計紀念碑是正確和符合史實的。 (央視新聞)

【福鼎市茶葉協會：銷燬近10噸、價值約200萬元的受災“泡水茶”】福鼎市茶葉協會官微9月6日訊息，近日，受颱風“沙德爾”帶來的極端強降雨影響，“中國白茶第一鎮”——福鼎市點頭鎮，遭遇了嚴重的內澇災害。9月5日，在點頭鎮的集中銷燬點，伴隨著熊熊烈火，近10噸、價值約200萬元的受災“泡水茶”被徹底焚燒。這是在開展受災問題茶葉集中統一銷燬行動，對所有因洪澇浸泡、受潮變質、不符合品質標準的福鼎白茶進行焚燬處置。此外，該協會發布《致全市茶企、茶葉生產經營者的倡議書》提出，受雨水浸泡、汙染的茶葉不得進入市場銷售，不得以任何形式流入消費環節。各企業要認真開展受災茶葉排查，對已被浸泡、汙染且不符合質量安全要求的茶葉，及時做好規範處置，自覺守住產品質量安全底線。

【U.S. economic pressure appears to be working; Iran's leverage in Strait of Hormuz wanes】After six months of conflict-driven market volatility, U.S. maritime blockades and tighter sanctions have materially curtailed Iranian oil exports and access to foreign exchange, leaving Tehran under one of the gravest economic squeezes in its history, according to Iranian and regional sources. U.S. and regional officials judge the pain could compel Iran to allow free navigation through the Strait of Hormuz, arguing Iran’s economic losses now exceed the disruption it can inflict. Measures to choke exports have reduced government revenue, and Tehran’s attempts to disrupt shipping have not triggered a sustained global shock: oil markets have re-priced and alternative supplies continue to flow. Iranian analyst Arash Aziz says the balance has tilted slightly against Iran—Tehran cannot fully close the strait and U.S. maritime pressure is having an effect. It is unclear whether the pressure will force concessions; Tehran has not abandoned its demands, but mediators are reportedly discussing a new proposal to break the impasse.

### 2026-09-06 21:06:45  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #The Fed (FOMC) #US10Y #Impact bearish #S&P500 #Impact mixed

【CITIC Securities: Market still range-bound; don’t panic over overseas rates】CITIC Securities says whether the Fed hikes in September is not decisive for long-end rates or equity direction. In an era of rapid AI development, demand for government bonds as a traditional safe asset is likely structurally declining; the sell-off in US and European sovereigns reflects economic and market dynamics, not a reliable signal for near-term equity moves. Overall, markets remain range-bound; investors should not panic over overseas rates or overreact to stronger-than-expected market responses to rate moves. The deeper driver of widening long-end domestic-foreign spreads is a capital supply–demand mismatch; with cross-border goods trade facing greater friction, resolving the impasse may depend on outbound financial flows.

### 2026-09-06 19:05:46  #其他

【預警視窗僅幾分鐘 吉隆泥石流災害鏈源頭在境外是監測最大短板】針對吉隆泥石流災害中堰塞湖、冰岩崩—泥石流鏈式災害等風險監測難題，中國科學院成都山地研究所研究員蘇鵬程在見面會上進行了解答。他表示，此次冰岩崩—泥石流災害鏈源頭位於尼泊爾境內藍塘裡壤峰北坡，在海拔約5200米處發生的冰岩崩高位快速墜落，沖刷山體形成巨型泥石流，沿普熱普藏布和東林藏布高速衝擊約22公里後直達海拔1800米左右的我國吉隆口岸，整個過程僅6～7分鐘時間，對下游造成極其嚴重的危害。蘇鵬程研究員結合吉隆口岸實際情況，建議未來可以加強以下幾個方面的工作：第一、強化喜馬拉雅山地區跨境冰岩崩—泥石流這類冰凍圈複合災害鏈成災機理研究，深化災害鏈的早期預警訊號研判和識別。第二，構建跨喜馬拉雅山地區空-天-地一體化的非接觸式監測體系，降低對極高海拔源區實地布站的依賴。第三，推進跨境科研合作與預警協同機制建設。冰凍圈複合災害鏈的發生是自然過程不受國界約束，上游危險源位於境外是本次監測最大短板。（央視新聞）

【衛星遙感監測顯示：東林藏布流域冰湖面積目前未見異常】9月6日，西藏自治區吉隆縣“8·26”泥石流災害應急救援指揮部舉行的中外媒體見面會上介紹，災害發生後，我國組織相關領域專家團隊和技術力量，對東林藏布流域面積大於0.1平方公里的11處冰湖持續展開衛星遙感監測，目前冰湖面積未見異常。近年來，西藏自治區基於多源遙感影像、外業調查及參考冰川相關研究資料資料，組織開展了西藏自治區冰川冰湖資源本底調查。調查顯示，共有冰湖3.3萬多個。西藏按照“重點突出、精準防控”原則，構建技防+人防一體化監測體系，優先選取187個高風險冰湖，開展衛星遙感監測，對其中49個高危冰湖進行地面監測和無人機偵測。建立氣象、水文、自然資源、應急等多部門資料共享機制。定期組織專家對冰湖風險進行會商研判。建立短臨預警“叫應”機制，制定冰湖緊急避險預案。

【明確養老機構重大事故隱患判定程式 兩項養老機構強制性國家標準釋出】市場監管總局近日釋出《養老機構重大事故隱患判定準則》強制性國家標準，該標準在總結全國養老機構安全管理經驗的基礎上，明確了養老機構重大事故隱患的判定程式，這套規則既科學又實用，能幫助一線管理人員迅速“對號入座”，精準識別風險。《養老機構重大事故隱患判定準則》，主要內容包括：範圍、規範性引用檔案、術語和定義、判定程式、判定規則、直接判定要素、綜合判定要素等7個部分。近日釋出的《養老機構基本規範》強制性國家標準將於2027年8月1日起實施。該標準對養老機構從設立、運營到退出的全週期應具備的安全與服務質量劃出底線。標準針對養老機構的選址與建築、設施與裝置、入院出院管理、基礎服務、安全管理、運營管理、評價與改進等7個方面，系統提出了90項具體要求。

【Kaiyuan Securities: Next-stage returns to come from intra-tech stock selection】Kaiyuan Securities: Short-term crowding has partially unwound and STAR 50 implied volatility has fallen, creating intra-tech segmentation opportunities. Strategy: maintain balanced tech exposure and use rebalancing. Rebalancing focus: small/micro caps (CSI 2000, Wind micro-cap stocks); sectors with upside net-profit surprises — electronics, defense & military, computers, oil & petrochemicals, nonferrous metals, non-bank financials; rotation targets — nonferrous, basic chemicals, new energy, agriculture, pharmaceuticals and selected midstream manufacturing such as shipbuilding; and high-dividend defensives (banks, utilities, power) for favorable risk/reward in volatile markets. Medium term: technology remains the main theme, broad beta gains will be harder and returns will increasingly come from within-tech stock selection. Positioning should target names combining a second-leg re-ignition and narrative traction: AI materials, domestic compute-power chain and upstream overseas compute-chain suppliers (PCB, optical-module upstream), programming/enterprise AI agents and applications, plus tech spillover beneficiaries including power equipment, power utilities, energy metals and liquid-cooling solutions.

【中色股份：控股子公司達瑞礦業收到印尼法院通知】中色股份(000758)9月6日公告，公司控股子公司中色印尼達瑞礦業有限公司（簡稱“達瑞礦業”）於9月4日收到印度尼西亞共和國雅加達行政法院電子法庭系統送達的訴訟案件通知，達瑞礦業成為案件第二介入被告。訴訟標的為：印度尼西亞共和國環境部部長/環境控制署署長籤批的2026年第1437號決定書，即《關於達瑞礦業在北蘇門答臘省達瑞縣SilimaPungga-Pungga鄉開展鉛鋅礦採礦活動的環境可行性決定（SKKL）》（簡稱“環境可行性決定批覆”）。訴訟請求為：要求被告暫緩執行2026年1437號環境可行性決定批覆；要求裁定環境可行性決定批覆無效或不產生法律效力；要求被告撤銷環境可行性決定批覆；要求被告承擔相關訴訟費用。截至目前，達瑞鉛鋅礦專案環境可行性決定批覆合法有效，該事項未對公司經營業績產生影響，訴訟最終結果和對公司未來經營業績的影響尚存在不確定性。公司認為達瑞鉛鋅礦專案對環境的影響已經進行了充分的分析論證並經專家評審透過，達瑞礦業將配合印度尼西亞共和國環境部積極應訴，堅決維護公司權益。

【水利部部長李國英赴西藏調研冰湖監測預警及風險防範工作】李國英指出，青藏高原冰湖分佈廣、數量多，且多位於江河源頭。受全球氣候變化影響，潰洩風險不斷累積，風險暴發往往引發鏈式災害，嚴重威脅下游安全。要全面摸清青藏高原冰湖情況，充分利用高分衛星遙感、無人機載雷達等現代化監測手段，科學佈設水文、地震動儀等監測設施，以及壩體位移形變、滲流滲壓、應力應變等智慧感測裝置，構建“天空地水工”一體化的綜合監測感知系統，精準掌握冰湖發展變化，及時預警潰洩風險。李國英強調，要堅持系統觀念，以流域為單元，深入分析冰湖潰洩的致險、承險、防險要素，充分考慮各環節風險鏈式疊加放大效應，“一湖一策”“一河一策”制定防範應對方案。要建立健全上下游風險隱患監測預警和響應聯動機制，落細落實鏈式災害風險防範各環節各方面責任和措施。

【AI short-drama production prices plunge, industry consolidation underway】AI short-form drama production has surged this year as technical barriers fell and capacity expanded, driving per-minute production quotes down from ~5,000 yuan to several hundred yuan—approaching cost—while bespoke projects still command 10,000–20,000 yuan/min. Key market rules: individuals who obtain household registration in Hainan may exercise Hainan resident rights to purchase commercial housing from the date of registration; active‑duty military serving in Hainan (officers, NCOs, conscripts) receive the same purchase rights; employees of companies registered in Hainan who actually work and live on the island may buy one commercial housing unit in Haikou; multi‑child families (two or more children) may purchase one additional unit beyond current permitted quotas. 【9月加息？美銀、花旗分歧明顯】全球投資者將緊盯美國8月通脹資料，因為這些資料可能決定美聯儲是否會在9月晚些時候加息。然而，華爾街主要機構對這份報告的解讀存在明顯分歧：美銀證券認為資料將足夠強勁，支援9月加息落地；花旗則預計核心通脹將進一步降溫，美聯儲更可能按兵不動。據追風交易臺，美銀證券預測美國8月核心CPI環比上漲0.22%，對應核心PCE約0.24%，年率將升至3.4%，認為這一水平足以說服美聯儲主席沃什通脹尚未得到充分控制，從而支援再度加息。然而，花旗預測美國8月核心CPI環比僅增0.184%，年率降至2.3%——若成真，將是2021年4月核心CPI突破2%以來的最低年率讀數，足以令大多數官員選擇維持利率不變。花旗指出，此次8月PCE（個人消費支出物價指數）資料的不確定性高於尋常——美聯儲對部分關鍵分項進行了方法論調整，這意味著CPI與PPI資料向PCE的常規對映關係在本月變得不夠清晰，同時過去五年的PCE歷史資料也將面臨修訂，預計將使核心PCE年率下調逾30個基點至3.0%左右。這一技術性因素增加了市場對最終PCE讀數的解讀難度。（券商中國）

【印尼一景區山火蔓延 我領館提醒中國公民暫停前往】9月6日，中國駐泗水總領館發文提醒中國公民關注伊真火山森林火情。宣告稱，近期，印尼東爪哇省伊真火山保護區發生森林及山地火情，火勢已蔓延至火山口核心徒步遊覽線路，火情持續發展、現場風險較高。根據東爪哇省自然資源保護局（BBKSDA）官方通知，自2026年9月4日起，伊真火山景區、登山步道及全部旅遊活動已全面臨時關閉，恢復開放時間另行通知。為切實保障領區中國公民人身安全，中國駐泗水總領館特此緊急提醒：一、立即暫停前往所有計劃前往外南夢、邦多沃索地區伊真火山景區的中國遊客、登山愛好者及出行人員取消行程、切勿前往。火情期間區域存在山體火情蔓延、濃煙窒息、道路封閉、救援受阻等多重安全隱患。二、及時調整行程已預訂伊真火山遊覽、登山、地接、交通住宿行程的公民，儘快聯絡旅行社、平臺、酒店及時退票、改期或改簽，避免滯留及不必要損失。三、嚴守當地規定當前景區全線封閉，嚴禁私自繞行、違規進山、夜間徒步等行為。密切關注當地消防、災害管理部門及景區官方釋出的解封資訊，待官方正式解禁後再安排出行。（央視新聞）

【無界動力張玉峰：具身智慧5年後有望對標成年人勞動能力】2026年亞布力論壇夏季年會於9月4-6日在成都召開。會議期間，新浪科技與無界動力創始人兼CEO張玉峰展開對話。他表示，如果將具身智慧與智慧駕駛行業做類比，當前的具身智慧大約處於2019年前後的自動駕駛階段。他預判5年後，具身智慧有機會達到一個成年人“面向勞動、能思考會勞動”的水平，並且可依賴、可信賴。在家庭場景方面，張玉峰認為，機器人達到成年人的能力大約需要5年時間，但這並不意味著5年後才能進家庭。“掃地機器人早早就進了家庭，把單一事情做得很好，它只需要泛化背景。最近大家想讓掃地機長出一個手臂，能泛化物體、撿襪子、撿積木。”他判斷，對於人形和半人形機器人，未來兩三年左右時間可以嘗試進入家庭，但需要3到5個殺手級技能。“比如把髒衣服拿到洗衣機裡、把乾淨的衣服拿出來晾上，這可能已經是一個殺手級應用。再比如把殘食倒掉、把盤子放進洗碗機......3到5個高頻剛需的事情，大機率兩三年內是可以做到的。”張玉峰說。（新浪科技）

【河南出臺15項金融舉措 精準支援城市更新行動】近日，國家金融監督管理總局河南監管局、中共河南省委金融委員會辦公室、河南省住房和城鄉建設廳聯合釋出《關於印發進一步加強金融支援城市更新有關措施的通知》（以下簡稱《通知》），推出15項支援措施，進一步健全多元化投融資方式，引導各類金融機構強化創新金融產品和服務，為城市更新行動提供全週期、全鏈條、全方位的高質量金融服務。聚焦群眾關切與城市所需，讓金融支援更加精準。針對城市更新涉及面廣、專案型別多、融資需求差異大等特點，《通知》聚焦五大領域分類施策，把更多的金融資源投向群眾最關心、城市最需要的領域。在服務完整社群方面，用好房地產“白名單”機制，加大對“好房子”的金融支援力度，支援收購存量商品房用作保障性住房，鼓勵創新個人信貸產品支援居民原拆原建、自主更新，降低居民籌資壓力。在市政基礎設施方面，圍繞老舊管網更新改造、“平急兩用”公共設施建設等專案加大中長期融資供給。在歷史文化資源保護利用方面，立足河南厚重歷史文化資源，統籌推進修復修繕和活化利用，強化金融要素保障，將歷史文化資源轉化為地方發展動能。在城市精細化治理方面，聚焦“微改造、微治理”場景開發面向社會運營主體的普惠金融服務。在新型城市基礎設施建設方面，引導金融機構將新型城市基礎設施建設專案、城市基礎設施生命線專案納入優先支援範圍，助力提升城市韌性水平。（河南釋出）

【湖南省老舊住房自主更新可獲財政獎補】4日，湖南省住房和城鄉建設廳、省自然資源廳、省財政廳聯合印發《關於支援老舊住房自主更新的通知》，支援湖南省城市（含縣城）建成區國有土地上符合條件的老舊住房自主更新，自當日起施行，有效期5年。 通知明確，適用於符合國土空間規劃要求且滿足下列條件之一的三類住房：產權清晰、不存在違法建設或違法建設已整治到位，經鑑定屬於C級、D級危險住房的；無獨立廚房、衛生間等非成套住宅；年久失修、安全隱患較大、使用功能不完善、配套設施不齊全的其他老舊住房。同時強調，嚴禁以自主更新名義違法違規拆除改造歷史文化街區、文物和歷史建築，不得變相建設別墅和私家豪華莊園。 產權保障方面，自主更新中形成的不動產具備分割條件的，可辦理分割審批與權屬登記，實行分割槽分層設權。剩餘土地使用年限較短的專案，支援結合專案審批同步辦理土地年限續期手續。對於歷史形成的無合法用地手續的建設用地，按不同時期依法分類處置。（湖南日報）

【中共湖北省委十二屆十二次全體會議舉行 省委常委會主持會議 關志鷗講話】中國共產黨湖北省第十二屆委員會第十二次全體會議，於2026年9月5日在武漢舉行。出席全會的有省委委員78人，候補省委委員1人。省紀律檢查委員會常務委員會委員、有關方面負責同志和部分省第十二次黨代會代表列席會議。

全會強調，要聚力科技創新，打造原始創新策源地。建強國家戰略科技力量，構建梯次銜接、運轉高效的實驗室體系，全面強化基礎研究，加快推進關鍵核心技術攻關，建設集展示、收儲、撮合、交易、轉化等功能於一體的光谷科技成果轉化中心，努力打造具有全國影響力的科技創新高地。

全會強調，要加快產業創新，打造現代化產業體系。最佳化提升汽車、鋼鐵、化工等傳統產業，培育壯大光電子資訊、生物醫藥及高階醫療器械、新型能源、時空地理資訊等新興產業，前瞻佈局量子科技、具身智慧、6G、腦機介面等未來產業。全面激發產業創新活力，培育更多具有全球競爭力的一流企業。

全會強調，要加強金融創新，打造科創金融品牌。實施“科技金融+”行動，構建全生命週期的多元化金融服務體系。深化“一鏈一金融”服務模式，鼓勵科技金融創新產品與服務在鄂先行先試。深化財政金融協同聯動，擴圍實施財政金融協同促內需一攬子政策。

全會強調，要推動區域創新，打造融合發展共同體。最佳化國土空間佈局，完善利益分配等合作新機制，促進武漢都市圈一體化協同融合發展，打造高質量發展增長極。以武漢都市圈帶動全省區域創新聯動發展，推動宜昌、襄陽、江漢平原等地打造各具特色的高質量發展增長點，推動十堰、恩施、神農架等地強化生態保護，實現生態優先、綠色發展。加強中部地區協同創新，主動融入全國創新鏈。

全會強調，要深化開放創新，打造內陸開放高地。實施花湖機場、天河機場“雙樞紐”提能工程，建設多式聯運國際物流樞紐。建好用好自貿區、綜保區、跨境電商綜試區等開放平臺，拓展對外合作新空間。深入實施“文體旅+”行動，發展文化創意產業，加快建設世界知名的文化旅遊目的地。

全會強調，要強化治理創新，打造一流營商環境。完善創新支援政策，持續加大科技投入力度。構建親清政商關係，做到“有呼必應、無事不擾”。深化“高效辦成一件事”改革，建好用好數智湖北“一張圖”，推動政務服務向“增值賦能”升級。持續推進大財政體系、國資國企、開發區管理體制、生態文明體制等改革。（湖北日報）

【Israeli military reportedly preparing partial withdrawal from southern Lebanon】Israeli sources on the 5th said the Israel Defense Forces are preparing a partial withdrawal from southern Lebanon and plan to establish a smaller "security zone" closer to the Israel–Lebanon border. The move follows a recent operation that destroyed Hezbollah tunnels beneath the Arita-hir highlands in the southern nebetiyeh area; under the proposal the IDF would reduce the depth of its control in southern Lebanon from about 10 km to roughly 3–4 km and replace the existing "yellow line" with a new "grey line." 【江西遂川湯湖鎮至高坪鎮明坑村道路全線搶通】9月5日上午，江西省吉安市遂川縣防汛抗旱指揮部通報，受颱風"沙德爾"帶來的連日強降雨影響，5日凌晨4時許，遂川縣高坪鎮明坑村石下組突發泥石流災害，造成12棟房屋受損，其中8棟完全損毀、4棟一般受損。目前，已救出3人（其中2人身體狀況良好、1人死亡），尚有11人失聯。經過連續奮戰，湯湖鎮至高坪鎮明坑村道路於6日凌晨1時30分全線搶通，目前正對全路段較狹窄處進行拓寬作業。從左安鎮、湯湖鎮往高坪鎮明坑村方向道路打通後，小車已可通行，搶險裝置正逐步進入災區。凌晨2時，3臺挖掘機已抵達明坑村現場前方指揮部投入作業。此外，隊伍調派動力舟橋、衝鋒舟、機械化橋等裝備趕赴現場，開闢水上救援通道，力爭儘快抵近核心受災點展開搜救。（央視新聞）

【4年銷售額增長近200倍 百年德企“追著金壇投”】9月2日，勞施保施新能源汽車系統整合（江蘇）有限公司在常州市金壇區開業。這是德國勞施保施集團在金壇投資佈局的二期專案，基地全面投產後將具備年產10萬套全主動懸架系統的生產能力，併成為賽力斯汽車（問界系列）的核心供應商。勞施保施總部位於德國巴伐利亞的塞爾布，擁有超過70年的電磁閥研發經驗和35年汽車配件供應經驗。其生產的空氣懸架電磁閥佔據全球市場80%至90%的份額，是細分領域的“隱形冠軍”。2022年勞施保施銷售額約500萬元，2026年預計接近9億元——4年間增長近200倍。勞施保施中國區執行長王紫楠透露，企業今年在手訂單突破10億元，超九成供給國內市場。公司正持續深耕中國新能源市場，加速本地化研發程序，未來將有更多產品在中國實現生產並供應全球。（新華日報）

【中國人民銀行原副行長朱民：中國經濟轉型為亞太提供新機遇】中國人民銀行原副行長、國際貨幣基金組織前副總裁朱民在發言中，闡述中國經濟結構轉型為亞太地區提供的創新開放合作新機遇。朱民指出，中國以科技創新為主要抓手，推出整體經濟調整政策，大力發展科技製造業和創新型消費服務業。上半年中國經濟增速達4.7%，科技產業和新服務業對經濟增長貢獻度達43%。中國推出全面AI戰略，在資料、算力等8個方面提出系統方案。新動能、新科技製造業貢獻上半年經濟增長的50%，鋰電池、積體電路製造等領域增速均超30%。在培育新興產業的同時，中國推進傳統產業數字化轉型。朱民強調，中國經濟結構改革正推動新的全面開放。開放不再侷限於產品和技術，而是轉向高標準制度性開放和營商環境國際對接。同時，在中國深度轉型和新的開放態勢下，亞太地區迎來6大合作機遇：一是推動數字化、智慧化轉型，加強人工智慧、量子科技、生物製造等前沿合作。二是加強產業對接，中國企業出海提升區域供應鏈穩健性。三是中國企業對外投資穩步增長，上半年直接投資達865億美元。四是促進技術普惠共享。五是共同推進綠色轉型。六是推進智慧時代能力建設、人才交流和資訊服務合作。（深圳特區報）

【德國運載火箭第二次發射成功進入軌道】當地時間9月5日晚，德國航天初創企業伊薩爾航空航天公司的“光譜”運載火箭從挪威安島航天港發射升空，併成功進入軌道。這是該型火箭的第二次飛行，也是首次攜帶實際載荷執行任務。此次飛行主要用於驗證火箭在實際飛行條件下的各項關鍵系統。火箭搭載了來自歐洲多所高校和企業的5顆立方星和1項實驗載荷。“光譜”是一款兩級運載火箭，高約28米，主要用於發射小型和中型衛星，最多可將約1噸載荷送入近地軌道。2025年3月，“光譜”火箭進行了首次試飛。火箭在升空約30秒後因飛行異常收到終止指令，隨後在失去動力的情況下墜入海中，未能進入軌道。此次成功入軌意味著歐洲商業航天公司首次從歐洲大陸發射軌道運載火箭併成功進入軌道。歐洲航天局此前表示，發展歐洲商業航天運輸服務有助於增強歐洲自主進入太空的能力。（央視新聞）

【EU sends Frontex patrol vessel to English Channel to support French migrant SAR】The European Border and Coast Guard Agency (Frontex) said it has for the first time deployed a patrol vessel to the English Channel to support French search-and-rescue operations for overloaded migrant boats. The vessel, provided by the Spanish Civil Guard, began operating this week near the French coast and will work with Frontex reconnaissance aircraft that have been deployed over the Channel since 2021. Frontex said the ship’s mandate is limited to supporting French SAR — not intercepting migrants — and will detect, identify and track small boats and relay real-time information to French authorities. Additional vessels from other EU member states are scheduled to join the operation in coming months.

【Northern cold air becomes active; many areas to hit H2 lows】With typhoon impacts on land waning, southern weather will stabilise. Northern cold air has become active; as an upper-level trough shifts east, early next week the cold air will cross the Great Wall, bringing widespread rain and a sharp chill. China’s Central Meteorological Observatory forecasts that from tomorrow through the 9th light-to-moderate rain, locally heavy, will move west-to-east across central–eastern northwest China, North China and north–central Northeast China. Temperatures in the Huanghuai region and areas northward are expected to fall 4–6°C, with parts of Inner Mongolia dropping 8–10°C. Daytime highs and nighttime lows across the north will largely mark their lowest levels since H2; forecast temperature maps show the north shifting from yellow to green.

【Temporary ceasefire around Zaporizhzhia nuclear plant to repair external power lines】IAEA Director General Grossi said on Sept 5 that, after agency-mediated talks, a local temporary ceasefire was implemented around the Zaporizhzhia nuclear plant to enable repairs to damaged transmission lines and restore external power. The plant lost its last external connection on Aug 20 due to military activity and has since relied on emergency diesel generators to provide cooling power for six reactors and spent fuel pools. The IAEA warned diesel reserves are running low and said that if external grid power is not restored or additional fuel cannot be delivered, the plant risks a full-station blackout within days. Under the ceasefire, Ukrainian technicians will de-mine affected areas before beginning repairs; IAEA staff will monitor the work. Grossi said this is the seventh similar temporary ceasefire he has negotiated since the Russia-Ukraine conflict to reduce nuclear safety risks.

【扎波羅熱核電站周邊實施臨時停火 以搶修外部供電線路】國際原子能機構總幹事格羅西9月5日表示，在該機構斡旋下，扎波羅熱核電站周邊再次實施區域性臨時停火，以便搶修受損輸電線路，恢復核電站外部供電。扎波羅熱核電站8月20日因軍事活動失去與最後一條外部輸電線路的連線，此後一直依靠應急柴油發電機為6座反應堆和存放使用過後核燃料的乏燃料池提供冷卻所需電力。國際原子能機構表示，目前核電站柴油儲備已經所剩不多，如果外部供電不能恢復或者無法及時運入更多燃料，未來數日內可能面臨全站斷電風險。根據臨時停火安排，烏克蘭技術人員將在完成相關區域排雷後開始維修輸電線路。國際原子能機構人員將對維修過程進行監測。格羅西表示，這是俄烏衝突以來他為降低扎波羅熱核電站核安全風險斡旋達成的第7次類似臨時停火。

【Chongqing unveils 16 measures to encourage foreign firms' onshore reinvestment】Chongqing's development and reform commission issued 16 measures to encourage foreign-invested enterprises to reinvest domestically, framed on an equal-treatment principle for domestic and foreign capital. The package centers on guiding foreign investment allocation, easing investment and operations, and strengthening safeguards. It explicitly targets participation in Chongqing's 33618 modern manufacturing cluster, prioritizing intelligent-connected NEVs, next-generation electronic information manufacturing, and advanced materials to strengthen and extend industrial chains. The city pledges to fully eliminate foreign-investment access restrictions in manufacturing; qualifying foreign-invested firms' onshore reinvestments in manufacturing projects will receive the same municipal-level project factor guarantees and support policies as domestic investors.

【中物聯大宗分會：進入“金九銀十”生產建設旺季，大宗商品市場有望延續穩中有進的發展態勢】中物聯大宗分會相關負責人表示，8月份指數環比回升，主要是國內外市場需求恢復帶動，企業生產經營活動加快，大宗商品市場景氣水平回升。進入“金九銀十”生產建設旺季，大宗商品市場有望延續穩中有進的發展態勢。但當前地緣政治、極端天氣等外部不確定因素較多，國內部分行業仍面臨有效需求不足、原材料價格上漲較快等問題，鞏固大宗商品市場平穩向好發展基礎，仍需加大逆週期調節力度，增強企業信心，做好形勢預判和輸入性風險防範工作。（央視新聞）

【需求回暖 8月大宗商品市場執行向好】中國物流與採購聯合會今天公佈8月份中國大宗商品價格指數。隨著國內外市場需求回暖，極端天氣影響減弱，部分行業供需關係改善，指數環比止跌回升，同比漲幅擴大。8月份中國大宗商品價格指數為132.3點，環比上漲2.8%，同比上漲18.4%。在中國物流與採購聯合會重點監測的50種大宗商品中，8月價格環比上漲的大宗商品有27種。其中，乙二醇、柴油和焦煤漲幅居前，環比分別上漲18.2%、10.1%和8.9%。（央視新聞）

【AI加速嵌入上市險企核心業務】隨著2026年半年報披露完畢，五大A股上市險企的AI（人工智慧）應用呈現出一個鮮明變化：AI正加快從客服、辦公等輔助環節向營銷、核保、理賠、風控等核心業務流程滲透。與此同時，“詞元消耗量”“呼叫次數”等指標開始進入險企半年報之中，部分險企已對外披露AI輔助銷售額、智慧稽核率等經營資料。 這一變化意味著，保險業AI應用正在邁過單純技術部署階段，逐步進入與經營管理深度融合的新階段。受訪專家表示，保險業務具有客戶規模大、流程鏈條長、稽核環節多等特點，這為AI規模化應用提供了豐富場景。隨著應用持續深入，AI的價值也將進一步向促進險企銷售、降低成本、強化風險管控等經營目標延伸。（證券日報）

### 2026-09-05 07:21:36  #市場 #其他

【打新陣營擴容 “兩創板”新股中籤率持續走低】若按照發行日期統計（下同），2026年以來已發行的創業板新股網上平均中籤率為0.0169%，這一數值較此前4年的平均網上中籤率水平均要低，一定程度上意味著今年發行的創業板新股較此前4年更難中籤。2022年至2025年4年間，各年創業板新股網上平均中籤率分別為0.0208%、0.0271%、0.0238%、0.0187%。

除了創業板新股中籤難度加大，近年來科創板新股中籤率整體也呈現走低之勢。資料顯示，自2019年科創板開板以來，其新股網上平均中籤率呈現一定起伏：2019年為0.0505%，2020年和2021年分別降至0.0385%和0.0317%。2022年隨著不少科創板新股破發，科創板新股網上平均中籤率回升至0.0409%，2023年進一步回升至0.0525%。此後兩年，科創板新股中籤率再度連續走低，2024年與2025年分別為0.0428%和0.0364%。

分析人士認為，新股中籤率的變化與新股市場表現、新股中籤收益高低等因素有一定關係，市場自身存在一定自我調節機制。（證券時報）

Brent Posts Strongest Weekly Gain Since July — Brent traded near $95.73 a barrel on Friday and was up more than 8% for the week, marking its strongest weekly performance since July, as the war in the Middle East continued with little prospects of peace. Iran and the US exchanged missile strikes this week, while Israel’s defense minister threatened “crippling” attacks on Iran’s infrastructure, including energy facilities. Meanwhile, US Vice President JD Vance said the US does not plan to hold peace talks with Iran until it stops attacking ships in Hormuz, adding to upward pressure on oil prices as Iran signals its determination to maintain a hard-line response to the latest US strikes. The EU has formally joined the US-led sanctions campaign against Iran, while South Korea said it is considering a military role. The rally could lose momentum if shipments through the Strait increase. Reports on Iraqi oil exports indicate an average of 2.35 million barrels per day in August, mostly shipped through southern routes.

Ibovespa Closes Lower as Fed Hike Bets Rise — The Ibovespa inched lower to close at 185,147 on Friday amid rising expectations for a US Fed rate hike in September. US payrolls increased by 162,000, about three times the consensus estimate, boosting rate hike bets. For Brazil, persistently high interest rates in the world’s largest economy narrow the policy path available to the BCB. The prospect of US interest rates remaining elevated for longer also reduces appetite for risk assets in emerging markets. Underperformers included Axia, down 0.8%, WEG, down 0.6%, and Rede D’Or. Petrobras lost 0.9% despite elevated oil prices, which remained volatile. Fnancial stocks gains limited losses, with Bradesco up 0.6%, Itaúsa adding 1.5%, BB Seguridade rising 1.1%, and Caixa advancing 1.1%. The latest Datafolha poll on the presidential race, released on Thursday, showed President Lula’s lead over Flávio Bolsonaro narrowing. Embraer rose 1.1% as Brazil posted a stronger-than-expected external trade surplus. The index rose 5.4% on the week.

【US August jobs lift Fed rate‑hike bets; Wall Street risk assets largely hold】US August payrolls beat expectations, reviving bets the Federal Reserve may tighten at its Sept. 16 meeting. US Treasuries sold off, the dollar strengthened and the S&P 500 fell on Friday but still logged a weekly gain. The bond repricing has not triggered a broad flight to safety: credit spreads remain near lows and pressure on corporate debt and equity indices is limited. JP Morgan says Treasury market liquidity has deteriorated materially, but corporate bond ETFs and equity index futures have not shown comparable stress. Market resilience is underpinned by solid growth and corporate earnings, with AI-driven capex supporting large tech investment. Traders are focused on the speed of any further yield rise rather than the single payrolls print; upcoming inflation data and whether the Fed revises its tightening path will drive the next moves. A rapid further increase in yields could force investors to reduce risk exposure.

TSX Closes Lower Following Jobs Data — The S&P/TSX Composite Index shed 0.3% to close at 36,514 on Friday following the release of Canadian and US employment data. Employment in Canada declined by 41,700 in August, missing expectations for a 15,000 increase. The weak labor data could support a more dovish stance from the BoC. However, despite keeping rates unchanged on Wednesday, Governor Macklem said policymakers were prepared to raise rates if inflation remained elevated. Meanwhile, US payrolls increased about three times the estimate, boosting expectations for a September Fed rate hike pressuring credit-sensitive stocks. Major banks posted losses, with TD Bank down 1% and Scotiabank shedding 0.9%. Gold prices declined, weighing on mining shares. Agnico Eagle lost 0.9%, Barrick shed 1.8%, and WPM retreated 0.9%. Technology stocks were mostly lower amid weakness among US hyperscalers, with Shopify edging lower and Constellation Software down 0.6%. In contrast, Celestica added 1.1%, tracking strength among chipmakers.

US Stocks Close Lower After Strong Jobs Report — US stock indices closed mostly lower on Friday after stronger-than-expected labor market data gave the Fed more room to raise interest rates. The S&P 500 lost 0.4%, the Dow shed 272 points, while the Nasdaq added 0.2%. Nonfarm payrolls increased by 162,000 from the previous month, above expectations for a gain of just over 50,000 jobs, in line with statements from FOMC members that the labor market is at full employment. Credit-sensitive sectors pulled back after the data as yields rebounded. Alphabet shed 1.1%, Microsoft lost 2%, Tesla tumbled 5.9%, and Palantir shed 4.5%, while JPMorgan retreated 0.9% and Visa fell 1%. Still, chipmakers rose after underperforming earlier in the week, helping to support the Nasdaq. Nvidia gained 0.8%, Micron rose 6.1%, Intel added 4.5%, and Marvell advanced 7% amid signs of optimism surrounding OpenAI’s new GPT model. Sandisk soared 11.9%, and AMD added 4.7%.

【美國財長預計戰爭結束後油價最低跌至40美元，並帶動國債收益率回落】美國財政部長斯科特·貝森特表示，一旦伊朗衝突結束，油價可能暴跌至每桶40美元，並帶動近期升至多年高位的債券收益率回落。貝森特在週五播出的一場與斯蒂夫·班農的訪談中表示：“等我們跨過這場伊朗衝突，我預計油價會降下來。此後石油市場將出現非常嚴重的供過於求。由於有大量新增供應上線，原油可能會跌到50美元，甚至40美元。” 貝森特說：“如果你去看利率，就會發現，它們與油價的相關性已經升至歷史最高。”貝森特還淡化了挪威主權財富基金擬削減美國國債持倉的重要性。彭博分析顯示，這一調整可能意味著該基金持有的美國國債減少750億美元。他說：“他們只是希望透過買入其他美國資產來提高收益率。他們想買房利美、房地美的債券，我對此非常支援。”

### 2026-09-05 03:12:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #Colombia #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Colombia PPI Growth Hits Lowest Since February — Colombia’s Producer Price Index increased 0.76% year-on-year in August 2026, easing from a 1.51% rise in July and marking the weakest annual increase since February. Agriculture, livestock and fishing was the only sector to record a variation above the overall average, at 6.80%. Industry rose 0.26%, while mining fell 6.59%, both below the overall 0.76% increase. The subcategories with the largest positive contributions were pome and stone fruits, which rose 22.07%, potatoes, which increased 129.82%, and citrus fruits, which gained 58.43%. In contrast, the main negative contributions came from crude petroleum and oils obtained from bituminous minerals, down 5.17%; milled or green coffee, down 19.09%; and coffee, down 18.03%. On a monthly basis, the PPI fell 0.33% in August. Year to date, the index increased 2.95%.

【US Justice Department suspends antitrust cooperation with Canada as trade dispute escalates】The US Department of Justice antitrust division was ordered this week to suspend all cooperation with the Canadian government, an internal email from Linda Marshall, head of international affairs, instructed staff to stop collaborating with Canadian counterparts on cases and policy matters; no reason was given. Such directives are unusual; US and Canadian competition agencies have long coordinated on auto-parts price-fixing, air-cargo probes and tech and aerospace M&A reviews. The pause comes amid rising US-Canada trade tensions after talks collapsed last month and the Trump administration imposed 50% tariffs on $20bn of Canadian goods; Canada responded with similar duties on some US products. The DOJ and the White House declined to comment.

### 2026-09-05 02:29:03  #公司 #觀點 #央行 #國際

【花旗推遲美聯儲降息預期，目前預計 2027 年將降息三次】花旗集團經濟學家在週五公佈強於預期的 8 月就業資料後，調整了美聯儲政策預期。他們預計 2027 年會有三次 25 個基點的降息；此前其預測為 2026 年 10 月、12 月以及 2027 年 1 月各降息 25 個基點。該行首席美國經濟學家安德魯・霍倫霍斯特在一份研報中表示：“我們此前預期的失業率季節性上行並未重現，這意味著美聯儲沒有降息的迫切理由。”花旗是去年對美聯儲政策預測準確度最高的機構之一，預判到央行三次 25 個基點降息，而多家大型同業機構當時預計美聯儲將維持利率不變。霍倫霍斯特寫道：“我們原本預計今年資料會重演殘留的季節性規律，失業率在夏季幾個月走高，進而催生降息的緊迫性。”報告指出，花旗依舊 “基於通脹放緩” 預期明年實施降息；對比最新預測，“風險呈雙向平衡，倘若勞動力市場走弱，或是人工智慧市場情緒降溫、股市下行，降息有可能提前到來。”

### 2026-09-05 02:28:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Brazil #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Brazil Trade Surplus Beats Forecasts — Brazil’s trade surplus widened 23.8% year-on-year to $7.39 billion in August 2026, above forecasts of $7.14 billion. Exports increased 12.2% to $33.16 billion, with agricultural exports rising 11.4%, extractive industry exports gaining 16.4%, and manufacturing exports increasing 10.2%. Export growth was mainly driven by higher sales of live animals, unroasted coffee, and soybeans in agriculture; copper ores and concentrates, nickel ores and concentrates, and crude petroleum in extractive industries; and poultry meat, soybean meal and other animal feed, and fuel oils in manufacturing. Imports rose 9.2% to $25.76 billion. Agricultural imports increased 7.4%, while manufacturing imports rose 13.4%. Imports by extractive industries fell 41.3%. Trade with the EU performed strongly, with exports rising 46.4%, while the trade surplus with China and Argentina narrowed and trade with the US resulted in a deficit.

【美國柴油零售價格創新高】美國成品油零售價格資訊服務機構“汽油夥伴”公司4日釋出的資料顯示，全美平均柴油零售價格3日創下歷史新高，突破2022年6月的紀錄。資料顯示，美國全國平均柴油售價3日升至每加侖5.848美元，超過2022年6月17日創下的每加侖5.819美元的高值，而去年同期全美平均柴油售價為每加侖3.7美元。“汽油夥伴”公司表示，雖然汽油價格經常受到新聞關注，但柴油對通脹和商業成本有更廣泛的影響。柴油為貨運卡車、火車、農業裝置和建築機械等提供動力，隨著柴油價格上漲，交通成本升高，通常導致消費品和服務價格上漲。“汽油夥伴”公司石油分析主管帕特里克·德哈恩說，柴油為經濟活動提供燃料，柴油價格創新高影響遠超運輸行業。供應鏈成本上漲會推高雜貨、居家用品、快遞服務等價格，給消費者帶來壓力。（新華社）

【Tesla Cybercab update disappoints; shares fall over 6% intraday】Tesla (TSLA.O) shares fell more than 6% intraday on Friday after its Cybercab robotaxi update failed to meet Wall Street expectations, raising questions about its ability to compete with Waymo in the U.S. robotaxi market. The company held an invite-only event in Austin on Thursday with no livestream and CEO Elon Musk absent, saying users could hail Cybercab in a limited Austin area via the Tesla Robotaxi app. RBC said the event disclosed little new information — pricing, production cadence and regulatory approvals remain unresolved — and Wells Fargo called the rollout disappointing, citing early operational issues in Austin. The U.S. National Highway Traffic Safety Administration has opened a review to determine whether Cybercab complies with federal safety standards and has completed required safety certification. Tesla shares had risen 5.4% the day before the event.

### 2026-09-05 01:52:54  #焦點 #國際

【報道：特朗普政府正在起草中東戰後方案】
據兩名美國官員及另外兩名知情訊息人士透露，特朗普政府正在制定一項戰後戰略，核心是依託地區力量遏制伊朗，並推動以色列與其周邊國家進一步實現關係正常化。
該方案仍處於起草初期，旨在伊朗戰爭結束後，以及特朗普任期最後兩年，指導美國的中東政策走向。兩項重大事件將對這份規劃形成制約：10 月 27 日以色列大選，以及 11 月美國中期選舉。
美國官員表示，這項戰略的初步工作，同步在特朗普政府高層政治層面以及多部門實務工作層面推進。
訊息人士稱，近幾周，特朗普總統已兩次召集高階顧問開會，討論該方案並徵集相關構想。
訊息人士透露，該戰略的備選框架包含三大核心方向：
1、在美國充當保障方的前提下，組建美國盟友地區聯盟，以此遏制伊朗。
2、落實特朗普加沙計劃的下一階段、促成以色列‑敘利亞安全協議、執行以色列‑黎巴嫩協議，合力推動 10 月 7 日襲擊所引發的地區衝突走向收尾。
3、拓展《亞伯拉罕協議》成果，達成沙特‑以色列關係正常化協議。
一名瞭解相關程序的訊息人士表示，這份新戰略還需要數週時間完成起草。“方案還在醞釀階段，尚未定稿。”
該訊息人士補充道：“這套構想意在把中東諸多事務統籌起來，規劃戰後地區聯盟的構建路徑。目前尚不能確定美國在中東的所有盟友都會予以支援。”

【美國製造業和建築業就業增長跑贏服務業 或許反映出AI投資熱潮的影響】美國製造業、建築業及其他商品生產行業的就業增長速度正超過規模更大的服務業，這可能反映出投資源源不斷湧入人工智慧建設領域所帶來的影響。美國勞工統計局的資料顯示，截至8月底的六個月裡，商品生產行業的就業人數增長0.6%，創2023年以來任何可比時期的最大增幅，也高於服務業0.4%的增幅。僅過去三個月，製造業就業人數就增加了43,000人，是2022年底以來的最強勁水平。這些資料有助於解釋為何8月份就業報告好於預期。當月非農就業人數創五個月來最大增長，失業率維持在4.1%。花旗集團經濟學家Veronica Clark表示，美國製造商近期的改善以及建築業好轉，很大程度上都與大規模推進人工智慧以及建設資料中心有關。她說，去年透過的《大而美法案》中的稅收條款推動了裝置和設施投資，為人工智慧以及資料中心建設提供了支撐。

Mexican Peso Hits Highest Level Since 2024 — The Mexican peso strengthened to around 16.89 per US dollar in September, reaching its highest level since May 2024. US nonfarm payrolls increased by 162,000 last month, well above market expectations for a 56,000 gain. The figures point to continued strength in the US labor market, which is positive for Mexican exports and the economy given the close trade relationship between the two countries. The trade war between the US and Canada, along with trade tensions involving other countries, has positioned Mexico relatively favorably in trade terms, despite the country also being affected by tariffs. Also, Mexico’s gross fixed investment rose 7.7% year-on-year in June, beating expectations for a 4.8% expansion. However, while the strong US jobs data supports economic stability, it boosted expectations for a September Fed rate hike, which would support the dollar and narrow the US-Mexico interest-rate differential.

【Canada's AI data-center push meets local opposition; Carney plan hit by project pauses】Prime Minister Carney's push to build AI infrastructure is encountering local resistance that has delayed projects and complicated federal investment plans. Oakville, Ontario and Rocky View County, Alberta have imposed one-year pauses on data-center projects, and Manitoba premier Wab Kinew vetoed a Winnipeg-area proposal. Residents cite high power consumption, noise and upward pressure on electricity rates. Ottawa on Thursday published a framework requiring projects to deliver long-term local benefits, limit environmental impacts and avoid passing power costs to households; Amazon, Meta and Anthropic have signed the principles. Canada currently has five large operational data centers and 159 announced or under-construction projects; the government continues to market cool climate, abundant energy and available land as competitive advantages while trying to address community energy, environmental and benefit-sharing concerns.

Gasoline Extends Gains — US gasoline futures rose above $3.15 a gallon, extending gains for a second session amid tightening fuel supply. Elevated tensions between the US and Iran remain an upward pressure, threatening further constraints to production and supply. Adding to the pressure, persistent attacks from Ukraine in Russian refineries pushed runs to multi-year lows. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories fell 1.173 million barrels in the week ending August 28th, reflecting continued drawdowns in recent weeks while US refiners are largely out of capacity to boost further production, with facilities in Midwest operating at over 103% capacity and some delaying maintenance to keep production elevated. On September 4th, the US national average regular gasoline edged higher to $4.15 a gallon, its highest price ever for September, according to AAA. Earlier in the week, President Trump met with fuel refiners in an effort to bring down gasoline prices, although no measures were announced.

Other key data includes Michigan consumer sentiment and existing home sales. In turn, Canada and Brazil will publish inflation rates. Meanwhile, the ECB will decide on interest rates, Germany will unveil industrial production, and the UK will release monthly GDP data. In Asia, a busy week will feature goods trade flows from China following renewed tariffs by the US, in addition to fresh CPI figures. Also, Japan is set to post its PPI, current account, and wages aggregates. Lastly, trade data from Taiwan could offer hints on AI infrastructure demand.

### 2026-09-05 00:35:05  #市場 #國際

【美國總統特朗普：增長不會導致通脹】美國總統特朗普在社交媒釋出有關美國經濟的帖子：這何其荒唐？我們剛剛拿到非常亮眼的就業資料，股市本應上漲，畢竟我們的信用狀況和經濟表現都在向好。然而過去 25 年來一貫如此：股市反而下跌。原因在於我們活在一種虛假現實之下 —— 僅僅出於對通脹的 “恐懼”，經濟形勢一好，就要 “扼殺” 增長。理應恰恰相反，25 年以前也一向如此。如果繼續奉行這套理論，我們國家就永遠無法獲得本應擁有的真正經濟輝煌。因為每當經濟向好，總有一群愚蠢的人想要立刻叫停這股強勁的上升勢頭。經濟增長並不會引發通脹！今早我一看到這份漂亮的就業資料就料到，股市本該像火箭一樣飆升，結果卻會下跌。我們的國內生產總值增速應當達到 15% 乃至 20%，而不是 2%、3%、4%，美國的經濟實力理應遠比現在強大。我們的債務將得以清償，各類利好局面也會隨之而來。記住，利率每升高一個百分點，美國每年就要多付出 6500 億美元的成本。我們理應享有全世界最低的利率，是我們帶動全球運轉，還為諸多原本發展無望的國家帶去巨大的經濟財富！

### 2026-09-05 00:23:26  #市場 #央行 #國際

【歐洲股市錄得四周來第三週下跌 美國就業報告強勁推升加息押注】歐洲股市錄得四周來第三週下跌，此前美國就業報告強於預期，交易員進一步加大了對美聯儲加息的押注。斯托克歐洲600指數收盤基本持平，本週累計下跌0.8%，創近兩個月最大單週跌幅。美國8月就業增長超出預期，失業率維持不變，顯示勞動力市場的動能強於此前認為的水平。交易員目前預計，美聯儲本月晚些時候加息的機率約為60%，而週四市場定價顯示加息機率還只有五五開。Ecofi股票基金經理Karen Georges表示，“現在幾乎沒有多少理由支援美聯儲採取鴿派立場。通脹正在上升，而資料也清楚顯示就業市場相當有韌性。”她表示，“市場目前並沒有太過關注宏觀經濟資料，注意力仍集中在第二季度的良好企業業績上，因此在這種情況下，好訊息就是好訊息。”汽車板塊表現領先，大眾汽車領漲。這家德國汽車製造商的監事會支援一項重整計劃，其中包括額外裁員5萬人，推動大眾汽車股價上漲6.5%。科技板塊漲幅最大，媒體和醫療保健板塊表現最為滯後。面對通脹前景和利率進一步上升的可能性，歐洲投資者態度趨於謹慎。市場目前已經幾乎完全計入歐洲央行下週加息的預期。

### 2026-09-05 00:21:29  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Platinum Rebounds From Two-Week Low — Platinum futures traded near $1,830 an ounce, after reaching its lowest level in two weeks, amid prospects of increased industrial demand. Hybrid vehicle demand remains resilient as volatile fuel prices dampen demand for internal-combustion-engine vehicles. The European Union is facing pressure to include tariffs on hybrid vehicles from Chinese manufacturers in the existing duties on Chinese fully electric vehicles. Additionally, a coalition of major US automakers, including General Motors, Toyota, and Volkswagen, urged lawmakers to permanently ban Chinese vehicles, as Chinese automakers are rapidly gaining market share across major economies, which could support demand for platinum used in catalytic converters. In the near-term, however, the metal remains influenced by investment flows following the latest US payrolls data, which reflected a resilient labor market, boosting expectations of a Fed rate hike and reducing demand for non-yielding platinum.

### 2026-09-05 00:19:28  #市場 #央行 #國際

【非農雖為鷹派增添籌碼 但美聯儲加息與否還得通脹報告拍板】美國上月就業人數意外大增，強化了美聯儲本月稍晚會議加息的理由，但加息仍非板上釘釘。8月非農就業人數增幅超過彭博調查中所有經濟學家的預期，失業率維持在4.1%。不過，最新報告並未顯示勞動力市場正在加劇價格壓力，分析人士仍預計，美聯儲下一次利率決定將取決於下週公佈的通脹資料。與此同時，美國總統唐納德·特朗普週五在社交媒體發文，再度向美聯儲施壓，要求降息。而投資者卻提高了對美聯儲本月加息的押注。BMO Capital Markets策略師Vail Hartman在給客戶的報告中寫道：“今天的資料為鷹派陣營提供了支援，但還不足以為9月16日加息提供決定性理由。雖然市場隱含的本月加息機率有所上升，但就業資料的重要性仍將低於通脹資料。”根據聯邦基金利率期貨定價，投資者預計本月加息的機率從約50%升至略高於60%。美聯儲官員一方面擔憂通脹持續偏高，另一方面對於貨幣政策短期內應如何應對仍存在分歧。新的價格壓力證據可能推動聯邦公開市場委員會傾向加息，而更溫和的通脹資料則可能令美聯儲繼續按兵不動。今年以來，美聯儲已連續五次會議維持利率不變。

CAC 40 Ends Week Lower — The CAC 40 inched 0.1% lower to close at 8,279 on Friday following the release of domestic and Eurozone economic data. Eurozone retail sales fell 0.6% in July, following a 0.2% increase in June and missing market expectations for a 0.3% rise. Luxury stocks closed mixed, with Hermes losing 1.6% and LVMH shedding 0.4%, while L’Oréal rose 1.4% and Kering edged 0.2% higher. France’s construction activity recorded its sharpest contraction since May 2020, while overall Eurozone construction activity contracted at a faster pace than in July. Despite this, Vinci gained 0.7%, while Eiffage edged 0.3% lower. TotalEnergies also weighed on the index, dropping 2.5% as oil prices pulled back. Dassault Systèmes tumbled 6.5%, tracking weakness in AI-exposed hyperscalers on Wall Street. Industrials were mostly higher. Legrand rose 3.3% after Deutsche Bank raised its price target to €160 from €150. STMicroelectronics gained 1.8%, tracking strength among US chipmakers. The index lost 1.3% on the week.

At the same time, the ECB is also expected to raise rates at next week’s meeting, which could weigh on credit demand for lenders. ### 2026-09-04 23:59:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #South Africa #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 0

South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield at 1-Week Low — South Africa’s 10-year government bond yield fell further to below 8.70%, reaching the lowest in a week, as the rand’s recent strength helped offset some of the inflation risks from higher oil prices. The stronger currency reduces imported inflation risks and boosts the appeal of the country’s relatively high-yielding bonds to foreign investors. The appeal of local debt has also been supported by the central bank's credibility, an improving fiscal outlook and efforts to boost structural reforms. However, elevated tensions in the Middle East have kept global oil prices high, raising concerns about renewed inflationary pressure. Domestically, recent fuel-price adjustments have also increased costs for households and businesses, adding to inflation risks. Against this backdrop, the South African Reserve Bank may be compelled to tighten policy this month after holding rates in April and July. Economists are increasingly anticipating a 25 bps rate hike on September 23.

烏克蘭總統澤連斯基4日在社交媒體發文稱，位於烏首都基輔市中心的烏國家安全域性總部當天遭俄軍無人機襲擊。烏克蘭媒體同日援引訊息人士的話報道稱，出於安全擔憂，計劃訪問俄羅斯和烏克蘭的美國總統特使威特科夫正在考慮代替訪問的其他方案。
澤連斯基稱，俄軍無人機擊中了位於聖索菲亞教堂對面的烏國家安全域性總部大樓，蓄意對局長辦公室實施打擊，烏國家緊急情況局等救援機構和執法部門正在現場開展工作。他已與烏國家安全域性局長亞歷山大·波克拉德通話並指示，一旦準備好，就對俄方進行“有力和對等回應”，烏軍方將提供支援。
烏克蘭外交部長瑟比加當天在社交媒體發文稱，俄軍襲擊烏國家安全域性總部是重大升級，需進行堅決回應。烏方呼籲國際夥伴對此進行譴責並加大對俄施壓力度。
烏克蘭《基輔獨立報》援引匿名訊息人士的話報道稱，“俄方不希望威特科夫和美國總統特朗普的女婿庫什納訪問基輔，他們被告知這裡（基輔）很危險”。目前，烏克蘭空域仍然關閉，威特科夫原計劃乘飛機前往，但現在訪問計劃可能有變。（新華社）

### 2026-09-04 23:17:26  #資料 #央行 #國際

【貝萊德：非農大超預期後 下週的通脹資料更顯關鍵】貝萊德的投資組合經理Jeff Rosenberg表示，美國8月就業增長大幅超出預期，在美聯儲決策者考慮是否需要加息之際，凸顯下週消費者價格指數的重要性。“這基本印證了我們此前對勞動力市場的判斷，也把焦點和壓力重新帶回到通脹上，”Rosenberg週五接受採訪時表示。“美聯儲是否會在9月的會議上加息，關鍵在於通脹是否上升，或者說通脹下降的速度是否不夠快。”Rosenberg表示，如果9月11日公佈的CPI繼續顯示通脹取得進展，“我認為他們會按兵不動”。“這份就業報告只有在異常疲軟的情況下，才有可能降低加息機率，”Rosenberg表示。週五的資料“更多是在為下週、為通脹資料鋪路”。Rosenberg表示，工資通脹已不再像新冠疫情時期供需錯位後那樣構成首要威脅，並將當前勞動力市場形容為“低招聘、低裁員”。他說，真正的通脹風險來自能源價格從整體通脹向核心通脹的傳導。就業報告公佈數小時前，美國汽車協會稱，美國加油站柴油平均價格升至創紀錄的每加侖5.85美元。Rosenberg表示，即使美聯儲加息25個基點，華爾街對股票和信貸資產的偏好也可能不會發生明顯變化。“股市更看重的是分子而不是分母，也就是說，更看重企業盈利、盈利增長，以及人工智慧科技熱潮展現出的驚人歷史性力量，”他說。他還表示，鑑於信貸質量保持強勁、利差維持低位，債市大機率也能承受一次25個基點的加息。

### 2026-09-04 23:16:00  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Currency #Importance 0

South African Rand Holds Firm — The South African rand traded below 16 per USD, hovering near its strongest level since the Iran war began in late February, amid growing expectations of rate hikes. Renewed flare-ups in the Middle East kept global oil prices elevated and prompted another sharp adjustment in domestic fuel prices. This is expected to add to inflation pressures and may lead the South African Reserve Bank (SARB) to raise rates later this month to defend its target range. South Africa's inflation rate fell to 4.3% in July from 5% in June, amid softer oil and food prices. The August's reading will be closely watched to assess the extent of price pressures. The SARB has so far resisted raising rates in response to the oil shock, holding rates in April and July. Meanwhile, speculation is growing that the central bank may deliver a 25 bps rate hike at its next meeting on September 23. Elsewhere, a stronger-than-expected US jobs report raised bets on a Fed September rate hike.

### 2026-09-04 23:05:50  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Canada 10-Year Yield Retreats After Jobs Data — Canada’s 10-year government bond yield eased slightly to 3.77% from a more than two-year high of 3.80% touched on September 3rd following the release of employment data. Employment in Canada declined by 41,700 in August, missing expectations for a 15,000 increase and following a 75,100 gain in July. The weak labor data could support a more dovish stance from the BoC. The BoC kept its key policy rate unchanged at 2.25% at its September meeting, as widely expected, but noted that inflation risks had increased while new tariffs had made the growth outlook more uncertain. However, Governor Macklem said policymakers were prepared to raise rates if inflation remained elevated. Meanwhile, US payrolls increased by 162,000, about three times the consensus estimate, boosting expectations for a September Fed rate hike.

Semiconductor ETFs netted about $3.3bn of inflows while DRAM-linked products recorded roughly $900m of net outflows—the first weekly outflow since launch. The team says this reflects profit-taking in memory and Korea-linked "new AI" names and a rotation into broader-coverage semiconductor stocks rather than an exit from AI exposure. Nasdaq-100 futures still showed near two-standard-deviation net buying, indicating overall tech risk appetite remains.

### 2026-09-04 22:04:10  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Baltic Dry Index Rises to Fresh 2021-Highs — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, extended its upward trend for a third session on Friday, climbing by 4% to a fresh high since October 2021 at 3,628 ponts. Shipping rates have risen sharply as strong demand for commodities, particularly iron ore and coal, meets tight vessel supply and longer routes caused by geopolitical disruptions. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes including iron ore and coal, also advanced for a third day, surging 6.4% to a new peak since December 2023 at 6,427 points. At the same time, the supramax index rose by 0.4% to 1,675 points. Meanwhile, the panamax index, which tracks vessels carrying around 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, halted a recent run of gains and fell 0.4% to 2,448 points. For the week, the benchmark index soared about 13.9%.

【lululemon商標案最新回應：雙雙提起二審上訴】9月4日，JU ACTIVE創始人黃皓銳在接受21世紀經濟報道記者採訪時表示：“我們早就提起二審上訴，今天收到資訊，lululemon方面也提起了上訴。”對此，記者也求證了lululemon方面，截至發稿時，尚未有回應。今年8月，江蘇省南京市中級人民法院對加拿大露露樂檬運動用品有限公司（lululemon）訴浙江極由品牌管理有限公司等五個主體的商標侵權糾紛案作出一審判決，駁回lululemon的全部商標侵權訴訟請求。涉案五被告共同運營JU ACTIVE運動服飾品牌，該品牌創立於2024年年底。不過，值得關注的是，在這起官司中，lululemon方面提出了兩方面的核心主張，其一是商標侵權，其二是不正當競爭。lululemon主張對其20個服飾款式享有競爭權益，認為JU ACTIVE仿製上述款式的行為違反了反不正當競爭法第二條的規定，構成不正當競爭行為。一審中，法院也確實認定了JU ACTIVE涉案的18個服飾款式設計構成不正當競爭，並判決JU ACTIVE賠償lululemon 260萬元。（21財經）

### 2026-09-04 21:47:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #Euro Area #Stock Market #Importance 0

European Stocks Attempt a Rebound — European stocks attempted to rebound on Friday following choppy morning trading, with the STOXX 50 edging higher while the STOXX 600 hovered around the flatline. Investors continued to assess the monetary policy outlook, with the ECB widely expected to raise borrowing costs by 25bps next week. Meanwhile, a stronger-than-expected US jobs report prompted investors to increase bets on a Fed rate hike, although the outcome of the September meeting remains a wild card. Technology stocks were among the top performers, led by ASML, which gained 1.5%, and Infineon, which rose 2.2%. Volkswagen jumped more than 8% to lead gains on the STOXX 600 after reaching a major turnaround agreement that helped avert an escalation with unions and shareholder Lower Saxony. On the other hand, Dassault Systemes (-4.4%), Vivendi (-5.3%) and Eni (-3.2%) were among the worst performers. For the week, the STOXX 50 fell 1.6%, while the STOXX 600 declined 0.9%.

【電力安全新規落地 將引導電力行業持續加大縣域電網建設】記者從國家能源局瞭解到，修訂後的《電力安全事故應急處置和調查處理條例》今日正式對外公佈，將於2027年1月1日起正式施行。國家能源局相關負責人對記者表示，縣域電網是聯通城鄉發展、守護民生福祉的關鍵基礎設施。此次《電力安全事故應急處置和調查處理條例》修訂，實現電力安全管控從聚焦城市電網到覆蓋全域城鄉的歷史性突破。當前，我國區域發展差異客觀存在，部分偏遠縣域仍面臨電網架構有待最佳化、裝置執行壓力偏大等問題。《條例》新增縣域停電管控條款，將充分發揮制度引領作用，引導電力行業持續加大縣域電網建設、運維及應急體系建設投入，加快推進城鄉電網規劃、建設、運維標準一體化。同時新規堅持實事求是，統籌安全投入與發展效益，因地制宜設定管控標準，兼顧各地發展實情。

TSX Edges Lower Following Jobs Data — The S&P/TSX Composite Index edged lower to trade near 36,500 on Friday following the release of domestic and US employment data. Employment in Canada declined by 41,700 in August, missing expectations for a 15,000 increase and following a 75,100 gain in July. The weak labor data could support a more dovish stance from the BoC. Gold prices declined, weighing on mining shares that have supported the index in recent months. Agnico Eagle, Barrick, and WPM each shed about 2%. Oil prices also fell, pressuring energy producers, with Canadian Natural down nearly 1%.

On the upside, Computacenter rallied 3.3% after UBS raised its price target, while Vodafone gained 2.6% after Goldman Sachs upgraded the stock to ‘buy’ from ‘sell’ and lifted its price target to 155p from 85p. ### 2026-09-04 21:24:10  #央行 #國際

【8月就業報告掃清加息障礙，下週通脹資料將決定美聯儲走向】美聯儲官員在 9 月 15‑16 日會議召開前明確表示，今年利率維持不變的背景下，通脹資料將是決定是否加息的首要依據。即便 8 月強勁的就業報告不會改變這一決策邏輯，但它消除了加息的一大阻礙。倘若在 7 月就業資料不及預期的基礎上，8 月就業繼續走弱，反對收緊貨幣政策的理由就會更加充分：為何要在勞動力市場毫無韌性的情況下加息？但週五公佈的就業報告將 7 月負增長的就業資料上修為正增長，並且六個月平均新增就業創下兩年多以來最高水平，上述反對理由已不再成立。儘管如此，就業與收入持續增長，也從另一個角度說明，利率或許幾乎沒有對經濟起到抑制作用。美聯儲理事克里斯托弗・沃勒週四表示，就業市場向好不會成為其利率決策的主要考量，但這一現狀，為評估政策在抑制通脹方面是否收效足夠提供了宏觀背景。美聯儲主席凱文・沃什上週稱，很難將整體金融環境定性為限制性狀態。勞動力市場仍在持續創造崗位，也降低了加息的代價：如果下週末即將出爐的 8 月通脹資料推動官員傾向於本月加息，那麼他們不必過度擔憂此舉會將本就脆弱的就業市場推向崩潰邊緣。由此，下週的通脹資料成為左右本次政策抉擇的核心變數。

Pound Near Two-Week Low as Strong US Jobs Data Lifts Dollar — The British pound weakened toward $1.35, hovering near a two-week low, as stronger-than-expected US employment data boosted the dollar and reinforced expectations for tighter Federal Reserve policy. US nonfarm payrolls rose by 162,000 in August, well above expectations for a 56,000 gain, prompting markets to price in a near 60% probability of a Fed rate hike this month. In the UK, markets are now fully pricing in a BoE rate hike by year-end, with another increase expected by March 2027. Bank of England Chief Economist Huw Pill said on Thursday that raising interest rates now could reduce the risk of the central bank having to tighten policy more aggressively later to contain inflation. For the September meeting, however, policymakers are widely expected to leave rates unchanged as they adopt a wait-and-see approach, assessing the trajectory of the conflict in the Middle East and its impact on energy prices and inflation.

Bund Yield Little-Changed Ahead of ECB Meeting — Germany’s 10-year Bund yield stabilized around 3.35% as investors assessed stronger-than-expected US employment data while positioning ahead of next week’s European Central Bank policy meeting. US nonfarm payrolls rose by 162,000 in August, significantly exceeding market expectations for a 56,000 increase and prompting markets to price in a near 60% probability of a Federal Reserve rate hike this month. In Europe, money markets continue to fully price in a 25-basis-point ECB rate hike to 2.5% next week, while assigning an almost 100% probability that the deposit rate will reach 3% by June 2027. This pricing implies two additional rate increases by mid-2027, keeping upward pressure on European borrowing costs. On the economic data front, Germany’s factory orders increased 2.5% in July, slowing from an upwardly revised 3.7% rise in June but comfortably exceeding market expectations for a 0.3% gain.

### 2026-09-04 21:11:43  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Currency #Importance 1

Canadian Dollar Weakens on Jobs Data — The Canadian dollar weakened to around 1.39 per USD following the release of domestic and US employment data. Employment in Canada declined by 41,700 in August, missing expectations for a 15,000 increase and following a 75,100 gain in July. The weak labor data could support a more dovish stance from the BoC. Meanwhile, US payrolls increased by 162,000, about three times the consensus estimate. The strong report boosted expectations for a September Fed rate hike. This backdrop could widen the interest-rate differential between the US and Canada, favoring the US dollar over the loonie. The BoC kept its key policy rate unchanged at 2.25% at its September meeting, as widely expected. However, it noted that inflation risks had increased, while new tariffs had made the growth outlook more uncertain. The BoC left the future path of monetary policy open, saying it would adjust policy as needed. Governor Macklem said policymakers were prepared to raise rates if inflation remained elevated.

### 2026-09-04 21:05:26  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 2

Treasury Yields Rise After Jobs Report — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note rose nearly 3 basis points to 4.79% on Friday, reversing declines in the previous two sessions following a stronger-than-expected jobs report. The US economy added 162K jobs in August, well above forecasts of 56K, while figures for the previous two months were also revised slightly higher. The report prompted investors to ramp up bets on a Fed rate hike, with markets now pricing in a nearly 52% chance of a 25bps increase in the federal funds rate this month. Earlier in the week, bonds sold off following a surge in oil prices and a pledge from Fed Chair Warsh to tame inflation, before recovering after Governor Waller said he would support keeping rates unchanged if inflation continues to move toward the 2% target. Still, the Fed’s September decision remains highly uncertain for investors, with inflation data due next week expected to provide further clues on the central bank’s next move.

### 2026-09-04 21:05:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 0

DAX Moves Higher — The DAX 40 rose further by 0.6% to around 26,140 on Friday, marking the second day of gains, powered by the automotive sector. Technology stocks and industrials also provided support. Volkswagen shares jumped over 8%, following the board’s approval of its biggest restructuring plan in 89 years. The plan includes 50,000 job cuts and alternatives for four German plants, while also reducing the supervisory board’s influence on key decisions, where unions and Lower Saxony hold a majority. BMW (2.5%, Continental (2.3%) and Mercedes-Benz Group (1.8%) also advanced solidly. Meanwhile, investors remained focused on the interest-rate outlook, with ongoing geopolitical tensions in the Middle East keeping sentiment cautious. The ECB is expected to raise rates by 25 basis points next week, while a hotter-than-expected US jobs report has also revived expectations for further rate hikes. The index was headed for a weekly decline of about 1.6%.

### 2026-09-04 21:02:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Silver Drops 3% as Strong US Jobs Data Boosts Rate Hike Bets — Silver fell 3% to below $65 an ounce on Friday as the dollar strengthened after stronger-than-expected US employment data reinforced expectations for tighter monetary policy. US nonfarm payrolls increased by 162,000 in August, following an upwardly revised gain of 23,000 in July and comfortably surpassing market expectations for a 56,000 increase. The unemployment rate remained unchanged at 4.1%, while annual wage growth eased to 3.1%, although the slowdown was less pronounced than anticipated. The resilient labor-market data strengthened the case for tighter monetary policy, with money markets now pricing in a near 60% probability of a Federal Reserve rate hike in September, according to the CME FedWatch Tool. The stronger dollar and rising rate expectations weighed on silver, which is particularly sensitive to changes in interest-rate and currency dynamics.

### 2026-09-04 20:57:55  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Stock Market #Importance 1

TSX Futures Edge Lower After Jobs Data — Canadian equity futures edged lower on Friday following the release of domestic and US employment data. Employment in Canada declined by 41,700 in August, missing expectations for a 15,000 increase and following a 75,100 gain in July. The weak labor data could support a more dovish stance from the BoC. However, despite keeping rates unchanged on Wednesday, Governor Macklem said policymakers were prepared to raise rates if inflation remained elevated. Meanwhile, US payrolls increased by 162,000, about three times the consensus estimate. The stronger-than-expected report boosted expectations for a September Fed rate hike, putting pressure on credit-sensitive stocks. Gold prices fell, weighing on mining shares that have supported the index in recent months. Oil prices also declined, pressuring energy producers while offering some relief on energy-driven inflation concerns.

US Stocks Inch Down After Jobs Report — US equity futures eased on Friday after evidence of a strong labor market added leeway for the Federal Reserve to raise interest rates to combat inflation. Contracts for the S&P 500, Dow, and Nasdaq 100 edged below the flatline. Nonfarm payrolls surged by 162 thousand from the previous month, well above expectations of a little over 50 thousand jobs, aligned with statements from FOMC members that the labor market is under full employment. Credit-sensitive sectors pulled back after the data as yields rebounded, with Microsoft, Tesla, and Mastercard dropping nearly 1%. Still, chip producers rose after underperforming this week, with Lam Research, Applied Materials, and Sandisk adding close to 3% premarket. On top of that, Marvell, Micron, Intel, and Nvidia jumped over 1% amid sings of optimism around OpenAI's new GPT model.

Canada Employment Unexpectedly Declines — Employment in Canada declined by 41,700 in August 2026, missing expectations for a 15,000 increase and following a 75,100 gain in July. Employment fell in business, building and other support services (-2.8%), public administration (-0.7%), natural resources (-2.3%), and utilities (-3.5%), while manufacturing employment increased 1.2%. By province, employment declined in Quebec (-0.4%) and edged down in Ontario (-0.2%), while it increased in New Brunswick (0.6%) and was little changed elsewhere. By age group, employment fell among youth aged 15 to 24 (-0.7%) and edged down among core-age workers aged 25 to 54 (-0.1%). Public-sector employment fell 0.4%, marking a third consecutive monthly decline, while private-sector employment was little changed. The employment rate decreased by 0.1 percentage point to 60.8%. Year-on-year, employment increased by 217,000 in August.

Canada Unemployment Rate Holds as Expected — The unemployment rate in Canada was unchanged from the previous month at 6.4% in August of 2026, aligned with market expectations. The number of unemployed people rose by 4.8 thousand from the previous month to 1.455 million in the period. 【8月美國就業資料亮眼，但求職群體處境依舊艱難】美國勞工部週五公佈，8月新增就業崗位16.2萬個，大幅高於市場預期。失業率維持4.1%，符合預期。經濟學家認為，美國已經進入一個新階段：無需每月創造大量崗位，就能維持勞動力市場穩定。人口正在老齡化——嬰兒潮一代中最年輕的群體今年已具備申領社保養老金資格；同時移民管控收緊，勞動力新增供給減少。換言之，美國沒有足夠的可用勞動力來實現大規模就業增長。美國銀行經濟學家估算，每月新增約2萬個崗位，就足以阻止失業率上行。從歷史標準來看，這是極低的就業增速。疫情爆發前25年間，月均新增就業約12萬個。與此同時，就業市場呈現這樣一種局面：企業很少裁員，但也不願大規模招新人。例如，首次申領失業救濟金人數處於歷史低位附近，而勞工部本週早些時候的資料顯示，招聘率同樣低迷。

US Job Growth Tops Forecasts — The US economy added 162K jobs in August 2026, the most in five months, following an upwardly revised 23K rise in July and much higher than market expectations of 56K. Employment increased in food services and drinking places by 59K and in local government education by 42K, largely offsetting a decrease in the prior month. Employment in manufacturing continued its upward trend (16K) and increases were also seen in employment in machinery manufacturing (6K) and in fabricated metal product manufacturing (6K). Employment in health care continued to trend up (13K), led by home health care services (11K) and hospitals (8K). On the other hand, information employment declined by 23K, due to computing infrastructure providers, data processing, web hosting, and related services (-8K), publishing industries (-7K), and in broadcasting and content providers (-5K). Meanwhile, the change in total nonfarm payroll employment for June and July was revised up by a combined 55K.

### 2026-09-04 20:16:34  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Private Investment YoY #Importance 0

Mexico Investment Growth Beats Expectations — Mexico’s gross fixed investment rose 7.7% year-on-year in June 2026, following a 1.1% increase in May and marking the third consecutive month of growth. The result exceeded market expectations of a 4.8% expansion. Overall investment growth was driven by an 18.1% increase in public investment and a 6% rise in private investment. Construction investment climbed 9.2%, supported by a 25.8% surge in public construction, lifting non-residential construction by 11.1%. Investment in machinery and equipment rose 6%, driven by higher imports, which increased 10.2%, led by a 15.6% rise in transport equipment and a 9.3% increase in other machinery and equipment. On a monthly basis, gross fixed investment rose 1.3% after falling 0.4% in May. In the first half of 2026, gross fixed investment increased 0.8% from the same period of 2025.

### 2026-09-04 19:44:26  #市場

【北京市“真金白銀”促餐飲超市提質，符合條件專案可獲補助】近日，北京市商務局正式釋出《2026—2027年促進餐飲、超市等業態高質量發展支援辦法》及年度申報通知。根據新政，北京將透過財政補助方式，重點支援餐飲、便利店進公園景區博物館、老字號餐飲振興、門店升級改造及中央廚房建設，單個專案依據審定實際支出給予一定比例資金支援。根據申報細則，專案所在地須在北京行政區域內，申報單位須為在京正常經營的企業。為避免資源分散，同一申報單位只能選取一個支援方向，且同一方向申報門店數量不得超過5個。在資金門檻上，單個餐飲門店或便利店審定支出不得低於20萬元，單個超市門店或中央廚房不得低於50萬元，且審定實際支出不得低於計劃支出的70%。

【U.S. Treasury volatility eases as markets await jobs report】After a turbulent week, U.S. Treasury yields were subdued on Friday as investors await a key U.S. employment report for guidance on the Fed's rate path. The 10-year yield was little changed at 4.76%, up about 5bps over the past five sessions. Early-week gains — driven by a spike in energy prices and hawkish remarks from Fed chair KEVIN WARSH — pushed the benchmark to a three-year high; prices later rebounded and yields eased after Fed governor WALLER on Thursday suggested inflation may moderate. Florian Ielpo, macro head at Lombard Odier Investment Management, said policy uncertainty is increasingly a driver of long-term bond volatility.

### 2026-09-04 19:43:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Up More Than 8% on the Week — Brent traded slightly lower around $95 a barrel on Friday but was up nearly 8% for the week, marking its strongest weekly performance since mid-July, as tensions between the US and Iran continued to drive market sentiment. US-Iran strikes resumed this week for the first time in about a month, reigniting concerns over supply disruptions. Israeli warnings that it would “cripple” Iran’s military and civilian infrastructure also weighed on sentiment. Meanwhile, US Vice President JD Vance said on Thursday that Washington does not plan to hold talks with Tehran unless Iran stops attacking commercial shipping in the Strait of Hormuz. However, there were signs that crude supplies were still reaching the market and Iraq’s oil exports rose in August and are expected to increase further in September. Meanwhile, OPEC+ is likely to keep its oil output policy unchanged for October at its meeting on Sunday.

US Futures Hold Rebound — US equity futures were muted on Friday, holding the prior session's rebound after dovish signals from Federal Reserve rate setters tempered concerns of a rate hike by the Federal Reserve. Contracts for the S&P 500, Dow, and Nasdaq 100 were near the flatline. The incoming jobs report is expected to show an unchanged unemployment rate at 4.1% and a rebound in payrolls, aligned with recent signals from FOMC members that the labor market is under full employment. Still, Fed Governor Waller had earlier stated that a rate hike is not necessary should underlying inflation gauges continue to slow, lending support to equities across all sectors. Chip producers led the gains after underperforming this week, with Lam Research, Applied Materials, and Sandisk adding close to 3% premarket. On top of that, Marvell, Micron, Intel, and Nvidia jumped over 1% amid sings of optimism around OpenAI's new GPT model.

【理想汽車回應26.5億元增資欣旺達動力：有助於雙方升級為長期利益共同體】9月4日，欣旺達電子公告，理想汽車擬以26.5億元增資其子公司欣旺達動力，認購其增資完成後8.79%的股份。交易完成後，理想汽車相關主體將間接合計持有欣旺達動力11.17%的股份。對此，理想汽車方面回應稱，本次投資有助於雙方從聯合研發、製造協同進一步升級為長期利益共同體。動力電池是高度專業化、重資產、重製造的產業。電芯製造不僅需要長期的材料、工藝、裝置、良率和質量管理積累，也需要規模化的產業協同能力。理想希望將更多資源投入到使用者真正可感知的技術突破上，例如大電量、5C 超充、長壽命、高安全、低溫效能和整車系統整合。透過戰略投資和深度協同，理想可以更深入地參與電芯技術、製造質量和長期能力建設，同時發揮產業鏈夥伴在製造端的專業優勢。這是一種更符合產業分工和長期效率的模式。

### 2026-09-04 19:12:01  #MKT News #Market Themes #China EV Leaders #NIO.O #Impact bearish

【NIO's Li Bin says auto sector in brutal final stage; profits under sustained pressure】NIO chairman and CEO Li Bin said the auto industry has entered the brutal final stage of a marathon, with sales and manufacturing margins at low levels and industry profits under sustained pressure. He said Q2 per-vehicle costs rose about 14,000 yuan versus end of last year, creating roughly 1.5 bln yuan of additional quarterly cost pressure. Q1 still benefited from some inventory buffering from last year, but Q2 shows the cost hit fully. He expects per-vehicle costs to rise a further 2,000–3,000 yuan in H2, bringing the cumulative increase versus end of last year to about 16,000–17,000 yuan. Li attributed roughly 10,000 yuan to higher memory prices, 3,000–4,000 yuan to battery costs and the remainder to commodity/material inflation, and said automakers have almost no room to negotiate these costs.

【Phison: NAND supply to remain tight next year; AI-driven semiconductor demand just beginning】Phison CEO Pan Jiancheng said meetings with two NAND suppliers found both admitting "we don't know how large next year's shortfall will be," indicating continued NAND supply tightness. He cited three market‑relevant drivers: recent relative stability in the Middle East has refocused U.S. attention on mediating the conflict, with U.S. domestic politics — notably the midterm run-up and pressure on Trump to claim a “peace” achievement — adding impetus; recent high‑intensity fighting has raised socio‑economic strain in both countries, reducing the marginal benefit of further large offensives; and both sides can use U.S. mediation to signal openness to dialogue, buy tactical respite, probe each other’s bottom lines, and position themselves for subsequent military and diplomatic moves.

### 2026-09-04 18:46:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ireland #GDP Growth Rate #Importance 1

Ireland Economy Expands at Fastest Pace Since 2021 — Ireland's economy expanded by 10.2% in the second quarter of 2026 compared to the prior quarter, following an upwardly revised 7.8% contraction in the previous quarter and exceeding preliminary estimates of a 3.9% rise. This marks the fastest pace of growth since the first quarter of 2021, driven by growth in the multinational-dominated sectors, which expanded by 11.2%. The globalized industry sector expanded by 22.1%, while the information and communication sector rose 2.3%. Meanwhile, the domestic sectors expanded at a slower pace of 0.7%, while personal spending rose 1%. The Modified Domestic Demand (MDD), a broad measure of underlying domestic activity that covers personal, government, and investment spending, contracted by 0.8%, driven by a decline in investment. On an annual basis, the economy contracted 0.4% following a 13.2% drop in the prior quarter, marking the third consecutive quarter of a contraction.
